<title>The Arrival, Five Ways</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,400&family=DM+Sans:wght@400;500;600&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
:root{
  --seal:#1A5E6B; --seal-deep:#14515C; --seal-tint:rgba(26,94,107,.10); --seal-ring:rgba(26,94,107,.32);
  --paper-0:#FAF7F1; --paper-1:#F3EFE6; --paper-2:#EAE4D8;
  --ink-1:#211C16; --ink-2:#4F473C; --ink-3:#7C7365; --ink-4:#665D50;
  --serif:"Fraunces",Georgia,serif; --sans:"DM Sans",system-ui,sans-serif; --mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace;
  color-scheme:light;
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark){
  :root:not([data-theme="light"]){
    --seal:#5FB0BC; --seal-deep:#7DC3CD; --seal-tint:rgba(95,176,188,.14); --seal-ring:rgba(95,176,188,.38);
    --paper-0:#15130F; --paper-1:#1D1813; --paper-2:#262019;
    --ink-1:#EFE7D9; --ink-2:#C0B6A5; --ink-3:#8E8576; --ink-4:#ABA294;
    color-scheme:dark;
  }
}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{
  --seal:#5FB0BC; --seal-deep:#7DC3CD; --seal-tint:rgba(95,176,188,.14); --seal-ring:rgba(95,176,188,.38);
  --paper-0:#15130F; --paper-1:#1D1813; --paper-2:#262019;
  --ink-1:#EFE7D9; --ink-2:#C0B6A5; --ink-3:#8E8576; --ink-4:#ABA294;
  color-scheme:dark;
}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
body{margin:0;background:var(--paper-1);color:var(--ink-1);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:14px;line-height:1.55}
a{color:var(--seal)}
a:focus-visible,button:focus-visible,textarea:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--seal);outline-offset:2px}
.wrap{max-width:1180px;margin:0 auto;padding:0 28px 96px}
.eyebrow{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.mast{display:flex;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;gap:24px;padding:28px 0 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.wordmark{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:22px;letter-spacing:-.01em;color:var(--ink-1);text-decoration:none}
.wordmark b{color:var(--seal);font-weight:600}
.mast .eyebrow{text-align:right}
h1{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:44px;line-height:1.05;letter-spacing:-.015em;margin:44px 0 14px;text-wrap:balance;max-width:16ch}
.lede{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-weight:400;font-size:19px;line-height:1.45;color:var(--ink-2);max-width:62ch;margin:0 0 36px}
.lede b{font-style:normal;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.contents{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:0;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);margin:0 0 48px}
.contents a{display:block;padding:14px 12px 16px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink-1);border-right:1px solid var(--paper-2);transition:background 160ms linear}
.contents a:last-child{border-right:0}
.contents a:hover{background:var(--paper-0)}
.contents .letter{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:26px;line-height:1;margin-bottom:6px}
.contents .name{font-weight:500;font-size:14px}
.contents .stake{color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12px;margin-top:4px;line-height:1.4}
.contents .verd{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--seal);margin-top:8px;min-height:14px}
.rule{height:0;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-top:2px;margin:56px 0 36px}
h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:30px;line-height:1.1;letter-spacing:-.01em;margin:8px 0 10px;text-wrap:balance}
h2 .letter{color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:400;margin-right:10px}
.idea{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:17px;line-height:1.45;color:var(--ink-2);max-width:66ch;margin:0 0 22px}
.cols{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:28px;margin:0 0 22px}
.cols h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:600}
.cols ol,.cols ul{margin:0;padding-left:18px;color:var(--ink-2);font-size:13px}
.cols li{margin:0 0 6px}
.cols li b{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:500}
.frame{position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:1440/900;overflow:hidden;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.frame iframe{position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:1440px;height:900px;border:0;transform-origin:top left;transform:scale(var(--s,1));background:var(--paper-0)}
.framebar{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:16px;margin:8px 0 18px;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.framebar a{font-weight:500}
.verdict{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:14px 20px;align-items:start;padding:16px 18px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.verdict .eyebrow{padding-top:9px}
.choices{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px}
.choices button{font:500 13px var(--sans);color:var(--ink-2);background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:7px 12px;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;transition:border-color 160ms linear,color 160ms linear,background 160ms linear}
.choices button:hover{border-color:var(--ink-3);color:var(--ink-1)}
.choices button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--seal-tint);border-color:var(--seal);color:var(--ink-1)}
.verdict textarea{width:100%;min-height:56px;font:13px/1.5 var(--sans);color:var(--ink-1);background:var(--paper-1);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);border-radius:12px;padding:9px 11px;resize:vertical}
.verdict .status{grid-column:2;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-3);min-height:14px}
.shared{margin:0 0 8px}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:13px}
th,td{text-align:left;vertical-align:top;padding:9px 12px 9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
th{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:600;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
td:first-child{color:var(--ink-3);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}
td b{font-weight:500}
.tablewrap{overflow-x:auto}
.foot{margin-top:56px;padding-top:18px;border-top:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-3);line-height:1.7}
@media (max-width:900px){.contents{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.contents a{border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}.cols{grid-template-columns:1fr}h1{font-size:34px}}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){*{transition:none!important}}
</style>

<div class="wrap">
  <header class="mast">
    <a class="wordmark" href="#top">Mudavym<b>.</b></a>
    <div class="eyebrow">Sketch 104 · onboarding directions · drawn 6 September 2026 · for the founder's pick</div>
  </header>

  <h1 id="top">The arrival, five ways</h1>
  <p class="lede">Five different shapes for a house's first hour with Mudavym. Every one asks the same five registers — <b>currency</b>, <b>what the house pours</b>, <b>vendor terms</b>, <b>what rings the phone</b>, and <b>the assistant</b> — and covers both doors of <code>/register</code>, the letter, and the first evidence. Each is live below: the Paper / Charcoal control inside a sketch works, the seal can be held, the steps can be walked. Example houses only.</p>

  <nav class="contents" aria-label="The five directions">
    <a href="#a"><div class="letter">A</div><div class="name">First evidence</div><div class="stake">the ledger row before the first setting</div><div class="verd" data-verd="a"></div></a>
    <a href="#b"><div class="letter">B</div><div class="name">The interview</div><div class="stake">the conversation lands as rows; one seal applies them</div><div class="verd" data-verd="b"></div></a>
    <a href="#c"><div class="letter">C</div><div class="name">The house's book</div><div class="stake">five ruled folios, each carrying its state</div><div class="verd" data-verd="c"></div></a>
    <a href="#d"><div class="letter">D</div><div class="name">The invitation</div><div class="stake">the house resolved before the account exists</div><div class="verd" data-verd="d"></div></a>
    <a href="#e"><div class="letter">E</div><div class="name">Set the table</div><div class="stake">everything laid at once, grey until confirmed</div><div class="verd" data-verd="e"></div></a>
  </nav>

  <!-- A -->
  <section id="a">
    <div class="eyebrow">Direction A · sequence · the seal lands when the first row enters the book</div>
    <h2><span class="letter">A</span>First evidence</h2>
    <p class="idea">The house's first ledger row is written before its first setting: one photograph of the last invoice through the door becomes the lines, a vendor, a price and a currency, and every register is then read from what that paper said and confirmed in place. Nothing is asked that the paper already answered; what it did not answer stays open, never defaulted.</p>
    <div class="cols">
      <div><h4>What it asks, in order</h4><ol>
        <li><b>Two doors</b> at <code>/register</code> — open a house (six fields, no currency question) or join one (the code resolves to house, city, inviter and role first).</li>
        <li><b>The letter</b> — "open it on the phone you will photograph with".</li>
        <li><b>The door</b> — photograph the last invoice; second road, the wine list. The 390 px frame is a manager doing this standing up.</li>
        <li><b>What the paper answered</b> — currency, vendor, terms, what the house pours, the low-stock line; each grey until confirmed; "Not now" recorded as a skip. One seal: "Enter it in the book".</li>
        <li><b>The book, opened</b> — one row, every register with its provenance; the nine producers offered with defaults kept; the assistant offered for what the paper left open.</li>
      </ol></div>
      <div><h4>Two roads not taken inside A</h4><ul>
        <li><b>Keep the currency question at <code>/register</code> too.</b> Safer when an invoice prints no currency, but it puts a setting before the evidence — the one thing A refuses. The address stays as the stated fallback.</li>
        <li><b>A seal per register.</b> Six ceremonies. The wax is rationed to the commitment that does not reverse in one click; only the row entering the book qualifies.</li>
      </ul></div>
      <div><h4>Honest gaps (drawn on the screen in mono)</h4><ul>
        <li>The skip writer does not exist — no route writes <code>configuration_step_skipped</code>.</li>
        <li>Payment terms are typed, not inferred: the extractor has no such field.</li>
        <li>Nothing matches the paper's vendor name to a provider; the person is the match.</li>
        <li>Register inference reads house items, never document lines — so "what it pours" can only be proposed after the lines become items.</li>
        <li>A house item is still a wine on this tree (ADR 0115 decided, not built); stock and cost differ by tree; the assistant's batch is not built.</li>
      </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame"><iframe src="direction-a.html" title="Direction A, live" loading="lazy"></iframe></div>
    <div class="framebar"><span>1440 wide, scaled to fit · scroll inside the frame · the Paper / Charcoal control is at the sketch's top right</span><a href="direction-a.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open A at full size</a></div>
    <div class="verdict" data-dir="a"><div class="eyebrow">Your call</div><div class="choices"><button type="button" data-v="keep">Keep</button><button type="button" data-v="rework">Rework</button><button type="button" data-v="merge">Merge into the winner</button><button type="button" data-v="out">Out</button></div><div class="eyebrow">Note</div><textarea placeholder="What to keep from A, what to lose."></textarea><div class="status"></div></div>
  </section>

  <!-- B -->
  <section id="b">
    <div class="rule"></div>
    <div class="eyebrow">Direction B · split screen · the seal lands on the batch</div>
    <h2><span class="letter">B</span>The interview</h2>
    <p class="idea">The arrival is one conversation whose every sentence lands beside it as rows carrying a reason and a provenance — typed · spoken · inferred · confirmed · defaulted · skipped · not yet answered · cannot be recorded — and one seal applies those rows item by item. Nothing the platform inferred is written before the seal, and a skip is a row too.</p>
    <div class="cols">
      <div><h4>What it asks, in order</h4><ol>
        <li><b>The door</b> — invited (the code resolves to "Hasan is expecting you at Lokanta Meyhane — as a manager") or opening a house; currency stated as a default in words.</li>
        <li><b>The letter</b> — the interview waits and says what is already on the server and what is not.</li>
        <li><b>The interview</b> at <code>/get-started</code> — the assistant names every source it read; the menu photo; what the house pours, confirmed by voice; vendors and their terms, one skipped; the nine producers; the low-stock line; consent for a person's own mailbox.</li>
        <li><b>The seal</b> — hold to apply; the receipt names each row written · refused · recorded · not attempted, and the date the seven-day undo ends.</li>
        <li><b>A joiner</b> arrives to the house as the owner left it, with what is still open, and may answer.</li>
      </ol></div>
      <div><h4>Two roads not taken inside B</h4><ul>
        <li><b>One register at a time, each with its own seal.</b> A sentence like "Anadolu — Tuesdays and Fridays, thirty days to pay" moves three registers at once; six ceremonies devalue the wax; a transcript without rows beside it hides provenance.</li>
        <li><b>The assistant before the account exists.</b> Rule 6 reads a house, and there is no house at <code>/register</code>; the door keeps the voice but is typed only.</li>
      </ul></div>
      <div><h4>Honest gaps</h4><ul>
        <li><code>config.propose_batch</code> is not built (ADR 0113 rule 5); the audit writer never sets <code>correlation_id</code> or <code>reason</code>.</li>
        <li><code>configuration_step_skipped</code> is not yet an audit action (one constant).</li>
        <li>Producers cannot be held one by one; the route carries five families per user.</li>
        <li>A vendor's usual currency has no field on the terms DTO.</li>
        <li>Voice: the speech provider is open (ADR 0113 Q6/Q7) — the microphone is drawn, not built. Google sign-up is D's road, not drawn here.</li>
      </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame"><iframe src="direction-b.html" title="Direction B, live" loading="lazy"></iframe></div>
    <div class="framebar"><span>1440 wide, scaled to fit · scroll inside the frame</span><a href="direction-b.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open B at full size</a></div>
    <div class="verdict" data-dir="b"><div class="eyebrow">Your call</div><div class="choices"><button type="button" data-v="keep">Keep</button><button type="button" data-v="rework">Rework</button><button type="button" data-v="merge">Merge into the winner</button><button type="button" data-v="out">Out</button></div><div class="eyebrow">Note</div><textarea placeholder="What to keep from B, what to lose."></textarea><div class="status"></div></div>
  </section>

  <!-- C -->
  <section id="c">
    <div class="rule"></div>
    <div class="eyebrow">Direction C · a bound book · the seal lands once, on the assistant's batch</div>
    <h2><span class="letter">C</span>The house's book</h2>
    <p class="idea">The arrival is the opening of the house's ledger: five ruled folios — Currency · What we pour · Whom we buy from · What we hear about · The assistant — behind a contents page that carries each folio's state (ruled off with the double rule when posted, open in grey when not, carried forward with a date when skipped, blocked with the reason) and what it unblocks. Never a percentage. Opened in any order, and remembered.</p>
    <div class="cols">
      <div><h4>What it asks, in order</h4><ol>
        <li><b>The flyleaf</b> at <code>/register</code> — who keeps the book and which house; currency deliberately not asked here; the code resolves to "Defne is expecting you at Lokanta Meyhane — as a manager".</li>
        <li><b>The letter</b> — the book is bound and closed until the emailed key opens it. No wax; it is the post.</li>
        <li><b>The contents</b> — opening stock (the wine list) first, then the folios in any order; fo. 3 blocked until a vendor is written; fo. 5 offered, never required.</li>
        <li><b>The phone frame</b> — fo. 3 for one vendor, written standing at the phone, one gesture.</li>
      </ol></div>
      <div><h4>Two roads not taken inside C</h4><ul>
        <li><b>Currency on the flyleaf, as today.</b> Kept off it so every register is one page with one place and one state; the house holds NULL between the flyleaf and fo. 1 and says so in words.</li>
        <li><b>One live spread with every page turning in place.</b> Drawn instead as one interactive spread plus four stills, each with the contents in that moment's state, so every page is seen without clicking.</li>
      </ul></div>
      <div><h4>Honest gaps</h4><ul>
        <li>The skip row is proposed, not built; today "Not yet" and "Confirm later" write nothing, so "offered 6 Sep · c/f" cannot be told from a page nobody opened.</li>
        <li>The assistant: <code>propose_batch</code>, provenance reads, <code>correlation_id</code> — none built.</li>
        <li>fo. 4 is per person, not per house; no per-producer switch.</li>
        <li>The book's memory has no single row — five reads, not one.</li>
        <li>Google sign-up unavailable; the low-stock threshold has no folio yet.</li>
      </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame"><iframe src="direction-c.html" title="Direction C, live" loading="lazy"></iframe></div>
    <div class="framebar"><span>1440 wide, scaled to fit · scroll inside the frame</span><a href="direction-c.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open C at full size</a></div>
    <div class="verdict" data-dir="c"><div class="eyebrow">Your call</div><div class="choices"><button type="button" data-v="keep">Keep</button><button type="button" data-v="rework">Rework</button><button type="button" data-v="merge">Merge into the winner</button><button type="button" data-v="out">Out</button></div><div class="eyebrow">Note</div><textarea placeholder="What to keep from C, what to lose."></textarea><div class="status"></div></div>
  </section>

  <!-- D -->
  <section id="d">
    <div class="rule"></div>
    <div class="eyebrow">Direction D · join-first · the seal lands once, at the founding of a house</div>
    <h2><span class="letter">D</span>The invitation</h2>
    <p class="idea">The house is resolved before the account exists: whoever was invited arrives already inside it, with everything the house has said about itself in view; whoever was not invited is founding a house and presses the seal once.</p>
    <div class="cols">
      <div><h4>What it asks, in order</h4><ol>
        <li><b>The door</b> — one question, "Who is expecting you?": an 8-character code, or "No invitation, I am founding a house".</li>
        <li><b>The invitation resolved</b> — "Hasan is expecting you at Lokanta Meyhane — as a manager", then name, email, password, one press. Signed-in visitors get a centred panel: "Add Lokanta Meyhane to your houses?"</li>
        <li><b>The joiner's arrival</b> — the house comes up as if signed in: answered registers with provenance, unanswered ones "not yet answered".</li>
        <li><b>The founding record</b> — one page: who, the house (address with its point, cuisine, currency defaulted in words, timezone), a read-back sentence, the seal held once. "What it pours" is deliberately not asked here — a register is read off the books, and the books do not exist yet.</li>
        <li><b>The letter</b> — founders only; the invited are vouched for.</li>
        <li><b>The owner's arrival</b> — first evidence, then the four registers each in a sheet, each skippable as a recorded fact; then issue an invitation, which closes the loop.</li>
      </ol></div>
      <div><h4>Two roads not taken inside D</h4><ul>
        <li><b>Bind the invitation to an email and pre-fill from it.</b> Would make "expecting you" literally true, but <code>targetEmail</code> is optional and unchecked today and the sharing model is the founder's open call.</li>
        <li><b>Ask "what it pours" inside the founding record.</b> The write needs a tenant and a verified session; a register read off nothing is a guess, which ADR 0108 refuses.</li>
      </ul></div>
      <div><h4>Honest gaps</h4><ul>
        <li>Join or found with Google — drawn in place, unavailable: both routes take a password; the Google route resolves existing accounts only.</li>
        <li>The recorded skip is not written anywhere.</li>
        <li>The joiner's progress route answers 404 — the arrival reads the registers directly.</li>
        <li>Expiry is not in the invite preview (the page prints "7 days" as a constant today).</li>
        <li><b>The inviter's name</b> on the unauthenticated preview is a founder fork — drawn, and flagged on the screen as such.</li>
      </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame"><iframe src="direction-d.html" title="Direction D, live" loading="lazy"></iframe></div>
    <div class="framebar"><span>1440 wide, scaled to fit · scroll inside the frame</span><a href="direction-d.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open D at full size</a></div>
    <div class="verdict" data-dir="d"><div class="eyebrow">Your call</div><div class="choices"><button type="button" data-v="keep">Keep</button><button type="button" data-v="rework">Rework</button><button type="button" data-v="merge">Merge into the winner</button><button type="button" data-v="out">Out</button></div><div class="eyebrow">Note</div><textarea placeholder="What to keep from D, what to lose."></textarea><div class="status"></div></div>
  </section>

  <!-- E -->
  <section id="e">
    <div class="rule"></div>
    <div class="eyebrow">Direction E · one screen · the seal lands at the foot of the table</div>
    <h2><span class="letter">E</span>Set the table</h2>
    <p class="idea">The whole arrival is one laid table: from a photograph of the wine list and one address the platform sets out what the house pours, buys, hears about and pays in, every inferred value grey with its source named until the person confirms it with one gesture, and one seal at the foot writes the lot item by item and hands back a receipt.</p>
    <div class="cols">
      <div><h4>What it asks, and the one sequence</h4><ol>
        <li><b>No order</b> — everything visible at once. The only sequence is physical: the account must exist before the list can be read, so "Sit down" is a record, not a ceremony, and the photograph waits on the table until then.</li>
        <li><b>Four settings in four states</b> in one still: Pays in confirmed; Pours grey with one line typed over the guess; Buys from typed with one term stated and four "not yet stated"; Hears about left — and the leaving recorded.</li>
        <li><b>Join by invite</b> is the same screen from another seat: the table read-only with Hasan's provenance on every row; the joiner's only place is name, email, password.</li>
        <li><b>The phone frame</b> — photographing the list at the pass and confirming Pours with one tap; the rest waits at the desk.</li>
      </ol></div>
      <div><h4>Two roads not taken inside E</h4><ul>
        <li><b>The seal at sit-down.</b> Rejected: the commitment on this screen is the configuration, not the account. Sit-down is a plain button; the wax lands once, at the foot.</li>
        <li><b>Confirm-all with the seal.</b> Rejected: a grey value nobody looked at would become the house's word under one thumb. Grey stays grey through the seal and is recorded as left.</li>
      </ul></div>
      <div><h4>Honest gaps</h4><ul>
        <li>"Lay it by talking" has no route — <code>propose_batch</code> is proposed, not built; said in mono on the control, the value and the receipt.</li>
        <li>No switch per producer; no vendor currency field; no batch undo yet (no <code>correlation_id</code> on audit rows).</li>
        <li>The list cannot be read before the house exists; a public extraction route would remove the only sequence on the page.</li>
        <li>No Google sign-up; the skip row and the receipt are drawn with the decided shape, neither writer wired today.</li>
      </ul></div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame"><iframe src="direction-e.html" title="Direction E, live" loading="lazy"></iframe></div>
    <div class="framebar"><span>1440 wide, scaled to fit · scroll inside the frame</span><a href="direction-e.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open E at full size</a></div>
    <div class="verdict" data-dir="e"><div class="eyebrow">Your call</div><div class="choices"><button type="button" data-v="keep">Keep</button><button type="button" data-v="rework">Rework</button><button type="button" data-v="merge">Merge into the winner</button><button type="button" data-v="out">Out</button></div><div class="eyebrow">Note</div><textarea placeholder="What to keep from E, what to lose."></textarea><div class="status"></div></div>
  </section>

  <!-- Elimination table -->
  <section id="compare">
    <div class="rule"></div>
    <div class="eyebrow">For elimination · what only each one does</div>
    <h2>Side by side</h2>
    <p class="idea shared">All five share the house rules: the skip is a recorded fact (no writer exists today — every sketch says so), the assistant proposes and only the seal applies (the batch route is not built), the wax lands once, absence reads "not yet answered", and Google sign-up is drawn unavailable because no route takes it. What differs is below.</p>
    <div class="tablewrap"><table>
      <thead><tr><th></th><th>What comes first</th><th>Where the seal lands</th><th>The shape</th><th>What only it does</th><th>What it costs</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr><td>A</td><td>The evidence: one invoice photographed at the door</td><td>The first row entering the book</td><td>A sequence of five screens</td><td>Configuration follows evidence; nothing is asked that the paper answered</td><td>A house with no invoice yet has nothing to start from; registers wait until lines become items</td></tr>
        <tr><td>B</td><td>The conversation</td><td>The batch of rows</td><td>Talk on the left, rows on the right</td><td>Voice and typing; the fullest provenance vocabulary; the receipt with the undo date</td><td>Depends most on the unbuilt assistant; the door stays typed</td></tr>
        <tr><td>C</td><td>The flyleaf, then any folio</td><td>fo. 5, the assistant's batch</td><td>A bound book with a contents page</td><td>State per page (ruled off · open · c/f with a date · blocked) and what it unblocks; any order, remembered</td><td>Its memory needs five reads today; the threshold has no folio</td></tr>
        <tr><td>D</td><td>The invitation code</td><td>The founding of a house</td><td>One door, then the record</td><td>The joiner arrives as if signed in; the founding record is one page with one hold</td><td>Founders still wait for the letter; the inviter's name is a fork</td></tr>
        <tr><td>E</td><td>Nothing — all at once</td><td>The foot of the table</td><td>One screen, mise en place</td><td>Four settings in four states in one still; the assistant is a control, not a flow</td><td>Dense at 1440; the list cannot be read before sit-down</td></tr>
      </tbody>
    </table></div>
  </section>

  <div class="foot">Sketch 104 · <code>.planning/sketches/104-onboarding-directions/</code> · branch <code>feat/mudavym-new-pages</code> · every route in the notes cited by file:line on 2026-09-06 · example houses Lokanta Meyhane and The Old Mill; Hasan, Defne, Marcus are invented · nothing under <code>apps/</code> changed. Your calls are saved to this page's record when saving is available in your view.</div>

<script>
(function(){
  // Scale each 1440-wide frame to its container.
  var frames=document.querySelectorAll('.frame');
  function fit(){frames.forEach(function(f){f.style.setProperty('--s',(f.clientWidth/1440).toFixed(4));});}
  fit(); window.addEventListener('resize',fit);

  // Verdicts, persisted in the artifact's record when available.
  var LABEL={keep:'Keep',rework:'Rework',merge:'Merge into the winner',out:'Out'};
  var blocks=document.querySelectorAll('.verdict');
  var dbp=null;
  function status(block,text){block.querySelector('.status').textContent=text;}
  function paint(dir,doc){
    var block=document.querySelector('.verdict[data-dir="'+dir+'"]'); if(!block) return;
    block.querySelectorAll('.choices button').forEach(function(b){b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(doc&&doc.verdict===b.dataset.v));});
    var ta=block.querySelector('textarea'); if(doc&&typeof doc.note==='string'&&document.activeElement!==ta) ta.value=doc.note;
    var chip=document.querySelector('.verd[data-verd="'+dir+'"]'); if(chip) chip.textContent=doc&&doc.verdict?LABEL[doc.verdict]||doc.verdict:'';
    if(doc&&doc.at) status(block,'saved '+new Date(doc.at).toLocaleString());
  }
  function save(dir,patch){
    var block=document.querySelector('.verdict[data-dir="'+dir+'"]');
    if(!dbp){status(block,'saving is unavailable in this view — tell me your call in the session'); return;}
    dbp.then(function(db){
      if(!db){status(block,'saving is unavailable in this view — tell me your call in the session'); return;}
      var body=Object.assign({},patch,{at:new Date().toISOString()});
      return db.doc('verdicts/'+dir).get().then(function(snap){
        var prev=snap&&snap.exists?snap.data():{};
        return db.doc('verdicts/'+dir).set(Object.assign({},prev,body));
      }).then(function(){status(block,'saved');}).catch(function(e){status(block,'not saved — '+(e&&e.message?e.message:'unknown'));});
    });
  }
  blocks.forEach(function(block){
    var dir=block.dataset.dir;
    block.querySelectorAll('.choices button').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){
      block.querySelectorAll('.choices button').forEach(function(x){x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(x===b));});
      save(dir,{verdict:b.dataset.v});
    });});
    var ta=block.querySelector('textarea'),t=null;
    ta.addEventListener('input',function(){clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(function(){save(dir,{note:ta.value});},900);});
  });
  if(window.claude&&typeof window.claude.use==='function'){
    dbp=window.claude.use('db');
    dbp.then(function(db){
      if(!db) return;
      db.collection('verdicts').onSnapshot(function(snap){snap.docs.forEach(function(d){var id=(d.id||d.path||'').split('/').pop();paint(id,d.data());});},function(){});
    });
  }
})();
</script>

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
<title>104 A &middot; First evidence</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,400&family=DM+Sans:wght@400;500;600&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
.mudavym{
  --seal:#1A5E6B; --seal-deep:#14515C; --seal-tint:rgba(26,94,107,.10); --seal-ring:rgba(26,94,107,.32);
  --paper-0:#FAF7F1; --paper-1:#F3EFE6; --paper-2:#EAE4D8;
  --ink-1:#211C16; --ink-2:#4F473C; --ink-3:#7C7365; --ink-4:#665D50;
  color-scheme:light;
}
.mudavym[data-ground="charcoal"]{
  --seal:#5FB0BC; --seal-deep:#7DC3CD; --seal-tint:rgba(95,176,188,.14); --seal-ring:rgba(95,176,188,.38);
  --paper-0:#15130F; --paper-1:#1D1813; --paper-2:#262019;
  --ink-1:#EFE7D9; --ink-2:#C0B6A5; --ink-3:#8E8576; --ink-4:#ABA294;
  color-scheme:dark;
}
:root{--serif:"Fraunces",Georgia,serif;--sans:"DM Sans",system-ui,sans-serif;--mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace}

/* motion, named from the house tokens */
:root{
  --settle:320ms cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1);
  --ink:160ms ease;
  --tuck:300ms cubic-bezier(.2,1.2,.3,1);
  --turn:420ms cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1);
  --pour:620ms linear;
  --stamp:360ms cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1);
  --tally:840ms cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1);
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){
  :root{--settle:0ms;--ink:0ms;--tuck:0ms;--turn:0ms;--pour:0ms;--stamp:0ms;--tally:0ms}
  *{transition:none !important;animation:none !important}
}

*{box-sizing:border-box}
html{background:var(--paper-0)}
body{margin:0;background:var(--paper-0);color:var(--ink-1);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;line-height:1.5}
.banner{background:var(--ink-1);color:var(--paper-0);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;padding:7px 20px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
.ground{display:flex;gap:10px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase}
.ground button{background:transparent;border:0;color:var(--paper-0);opacity:.55;font:inherit;letter-spacing:inherit;text-transform:inherit;cursor:pointer;padding:2px 0;border-bottom:1px solid transparent}
.ground button[aria-pressed="true"]{opacity:1;border-bottom-color:var(--paper-0)}
.wrap{max-width:1400px;margin:0 auto;padding:22px 20px 90px}
.wordmark{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:14px;letter-spacing:.03em}
.wordmark .dot{color:var(--seal)}
h1{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:34px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.018em;line-height:1.08;margin:6px 0 0}
.lede{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:15.5px;color:var(--ink-2);margin:8px 0 0;max-width:960px}
.dbl{border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);height:3px;opacity:.5;margin:18px 0 0}
.eyebrow{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.eyebrow.seal{color:var(--seal-deep)}
p{color:var(--ink-2);margin:6px 0 0;max-width:900px}
b{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:600}
code,.mono{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.02em}
.note{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.02em}
.note b{color:var(--ink-2)}

/* the flow strip */
.flow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);margin:16px 0 0;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1)}
.flow .st{padding:12px 16px;border-right:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.flow .st:last-child{border-right:0}
.flow .n{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.flow .t{font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1);margin-top:3px}
.flow .c{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:3px}
.flow .st.evid{background:var(--seal-tint);border-left:2px solid var(--seal)}

/* screens */
.sec{margin:40px 0 0}
.sec-h{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:12px}
.sec-n{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--seal-deep);white-space:nowrap}
h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:24px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.012em;margin:0}
.sec-k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin:6px 0 12px}
.screen{border:1px solid var(--ink-1);background:var(--paper-0);position:relative}
.screen .cap{position:absolute;top:-9px;left:14px;background:var(--paper-0);padding:0 6px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.app-h{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:16px 28px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.app-h .right{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.app-h .right a{color:var(--ink-2);text-decoration:none;margin-left:16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.title{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:27px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.014em;color:var(--ink-1);line-height:1.15}
.ask{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:15px;color:var(--ink-2);margin-top:6px}

/* forms */
.field{margin-top:12px}
.field label{display:block;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink-2);margin-bottom:4px}
.in{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);color:var(--ink-1);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:14px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:2px;width:100%}
.in.mono{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:20px;letter-spacing:.28em;text-align:center;color:var(--ink-1)}
.in:focus{outline:2px solid var(--seal-ring);outline-offset:1px}
.help{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px}
.btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;border:1px solid var(--seal);background:var(--seal);color:var(--paper-0);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;padding:11px 18px;border-radius:2px;cursor:pointer;transition:background var(--ink),color var(--ink)}
.btn.dry{background:transparent;color:var(--seal-deep)}
.btn.plain{border-color:var(--paper-2);background:transparent;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500}
.btn.off{border-color:var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);color:var(--ink-3);cursor:default}
.btn:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--seal-ring);outline-offset:2px}
.link{color:var(--seal-deep);text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px solid var(--seal-ring);font-weight:500}

/* screen 1: two paths */
.paths{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1px 1fr;gap:0}
.path{padding:26px 32px 28px}
.vr{background:var(--paper-2)}
.resolved{margin-top:14px;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);border-left:2px solid var(--seal);background:var(--seal-tint);padding:12px 14px}
.resolved .who{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:16px;color:var(--ink-1)}
.resolved .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px}
.notasked{margin-top:16px;border-top:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding-top:12px}
.notasked li{color:var(--ink-3);font-size:12px;margin-top:3px}
.notasked li b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500}

/* screen 2: verify */
.verify{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr 1fr;gap:32px;padding:30px 32px 32px;align-items:center}
.mail{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:16px 18px}
.mail .from{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase}
.mail .subj{font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1);margin-top:6px}
.mail .body{color:var(--ink-2);margin-top:8px;font-size:12.5px}
.mail .body .lnk{display:inline-block;margin-top:10px;border:1px solid var(--seal);color:var(--seal-deep);padding:7px 12px;font-weight:600;border-radius:2px}

/* screen 3: the door */
.door{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 390px;gap:28px;align-items:start}
.door-main{padding:30px 32px 32px}
.choices{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.4fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-top:18px}
.choice{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:18px 20px;border-radius:2px;transition:border-color var(--ink)}
.choice.first{border-color:var(--seal);border-width:1px;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--seal-ring)}
.choice .h{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:18px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.choice .s{color:var(--ink-2);margin-top:6px;font-size:12.5px}
.choice .r{margin-top:12px}
.ico{width:15px;height:15px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:1.7;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round;vertical-align:-3px}
.phone{width:390px;border:1px solid var(--ink-1);border-radius:26px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--paper-0);position:relative}
.phone .cap{left:18px}
.ph-h{padding:14px 18px 10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline}
.ph-h .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:19px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.01em}
.finder{margin:0 14px;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);height:330px;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
.finder .corner{position:absolute;width:26px;height:26px;border:2px solid var(--seal);border-radius:2px}
.finder .c1{top:14px;left:14px;border-right:0;border-bottom:0}
.finder .c2{top:14px;right:14px;border-left:0;border-bottom:0}
.finder .c3{bottom:14px;left:14px;border-right:0;border-top:0}
.finder .c4{bottom:14px;right:14px;border-left:0;border-top:0}
.sheet{position:absolute;left:40px;right:40px;top:34px;bottom:34px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:16px 14px;transform:rotate(-1.5deg)}
.sheet .l{height:6px;background:var(--paper-2);margin-top:8px;border-radius:1px}
.sheet .l.s{width:55%}
.sheet .l.m{width:80%}
.sheet .l.t{width:40%;background:var(--ink-3);opacity:.5}
.ph-f{padding:14px 18px 18px}
.ph-f .btn{width:100%;justify-content:center;padding:14px}
.ph-f .help{text-align:center;margin-top:8px}
.ph-read{padding:0 18px 18px}
.ph-line{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-size:12px}
.ph-line .q{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-2);white-space:nowrap}

/* screen 4: the paper and the registers it answered */
.read{display:grid;grid-template-columns:400px 1fr;gap:0}
.paper{border-right:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:22px 24px;background:var(--paper-1)}
.paper .photo{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-0);padding:18px 16px;transform:rotate(-.6deg);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);line-height:1.7}
.paper .photo .v{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:600}
.paper .photo .row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px}
.paper .photo .seen{border:1px solid var(--seal);color:var(--seal-deep);padding:0 3px;border-radius:2px}
.doc{padding:22px 28px 26px}
.doc-h{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;gap:16px}
.doc-h .v{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:22px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.doc-h .m{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.06em;text-align:right}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin-top:14px;font-size:12.5px}
th{text-align:left;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:400;padding:0 10px 6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
td{padding:8px 10px 8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);color:var(--ink-2);vertical-align:top}
td.n,th.n{text-align:right;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11.5px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;white-space:nowrap}
td .rg{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
tr.sub td{border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
tr.tot td{border-bottom:3px double var(--ink-1);color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:600}
tr.tot td.n{font-size:14px}
.prov{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.04em}
.inf{color:var(--ink-3);border-bottom:1px dashed var(--ink-3)}

.regs{margin-top:22px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.reg{display:grid;grid-template-columns:150px 1fr 300px 190px;gap:18px;padding:14px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);align-items:start}
.reg .nm{font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.reg .nm .r{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:3px;font-weight:400}
.reg .said{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2)}
.reg .said .q{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3);display:block;margin-top:3px}
.reg .val{font-size:13px}
.reg .val .v{color:var(--ink-3);border-bottom:1px dashed var(--ink-3);transition:color var(--ink),border-color var(--ink)}
.reg .val .p{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px;letter-spacing:.03em}
.reg.confirmed .val .v{color:var(--ink-1);border-bottom-color:transparent}
.reg.skipped .val .v{color:var(--ink-3);border-bottom-color:transparent;font-style:italic}
.reg .act{display:flex;gap:6px;justify-content:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.reg .act .btn{padding:8px 12px;font-size:12px}
.reg .act .chg{display:none}
.reg.confirmed .act .yes,.reg.confirmed .act .no,.reg.skipped .act .yes,.reg.skipped .act .no{display:none}
.reg.confirmed .act .chg,.reg.skipped .act .chg{display:inline-flex}
.reg .val{animation:settle var(--settle) both}
@keyframes settle{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(4px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}
.audit{display:none;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:12px 16px;margin:12px 0 0;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;line-height:1.75;color:var(--ink-2)}
.audit.on{display:block;animation:settle var(--settle) both}
.audit .kk{color:var(--seal-deep)}
.audit .vv{color:var(--ink-1)}
.gap{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.03em;border-left:2px solid var(--ink-3);padding-left:8px;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.6}

/* the popover specimen (320px, anchored, no dim, close is words) */
.pop-anchor{position:relative}
.pop{position:absolute;top:34px;left:0;width:320px;border:1px solid var(--ink-1);background:var(--paper-0);padding:14px 16px;z-index:2;box-shadow:0 12px 30px -18px var(--ink-1);animation:settle var(--settle) both}
.pop .h{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:15px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.pop .opt{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-2)}
.pop .opt .c{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.pop .opt.on{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:600}
.pop .close{margin-top:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
.pop .close .btn{padding:7px 10px;font-size:12px}
.pop .cap{position:static;padding:0;display:block;margin-bottom:6px}

/* the seal */
.commit{margin-top:22px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-top:18px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 360px;gap:24px;align-items:start}
.commit .say{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:19px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1);line-height:1.3}
.commit .sub{color:var(--ink-2);margin-top:6px;font-size:12.5px;max-width:560px}
.hold{position:relative;border:1px solid var(--seal);background:transparent;color:var(--seal-deep);font-family:var(--sans);font-weight:600;font-size:14px;padding:16px 18px;border-radius:2px;width:100%;text-align:left;cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none}
.hold .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;width:0;background:var(--seal);opacity:.16;transition:none}
.hold.pouring .fill{width:100%;transition:width var(--pour)}
.hold .lbl{position:relative;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px}
.hold .lbl .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:400}
.hold:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--seal-ring);outline-offset:2px}
.hold.done{border-color:var(--paper-2);color:var(--ink-3);cursor:default}
.hold.done .fill{width:0}
.stamped{display:none;margin-top:14px;align-items:flex-start;gap:14px}
.stamped.on{display:flex}
.stamped svg{flex:none;animation:stamp var(--stamp) both}
@keyframes stamp{from{transform:scale(1.5) rotate(-8deg);opacity:0}70%{transform:scale(.96) rotate(1deg);opacity:1}to{transform:none;opacity:1}}
.stamped .rb{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:15px;color:var(--ink-1);line-height:1.45}
.stamped .rb .m{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-style:normal;font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:6px}
.early{display:none;margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.early.on{display:block}
.writes{margin-top:10px}
.writes li{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-3);line-height:1.7}
.writes li b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600}

/* screen 5: the book */
.book{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.25fr 1fr;gap:0}
.ledger{padding:24px 28px 26px;border-right:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.row1{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:14px 16px;margin-top:12px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;gap:16px;align-items:baseline}
.row1 .d{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.row1 .w{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:600}
.row1 .w small{display:block;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2);font-size:12px;margin-top:2px}
.row1 .a{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-1);border-bottom:3px double var(--ink-1);padding-bottom:2px;white-space:nowrap}
.stock{margin-top:10px;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2)}
.states{padding:24px 28px 26px}
.state{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:14px;padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-size:12.5px;align-items:baseline}
.state .n{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:500}
.state .n small{display:block;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-3);font-size:11px}
.state .v{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--ink-3);text-align:right;max-width:58%}
.state .v.ink{color:var(--ink-1)}
.state.ruled .v{border-bottom:3px double var(--ink-1);padding-bottom:2px}
.open-q{margin-top:22px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-top:16px}
.nine{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:8px;margin-top:10px}
.pr{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:9px 11px;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px}
.pr .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase}
.assist{margin-top:14px;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:14px 16px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:16px;align-items:center}
.assist .h{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:16px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1)}
.assist .s{color:var(--ink-2);font-size:12.5px;margin-top:4px}

/* two roads + cite */
.two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px;margin-top:14px}
.panel{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:14px 16px}
.panel .h{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.panel .big{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:16px;font-weight:600;color:var(--ink-1);margin-top:6px;line-height:1.35}
.panel p{font-size:12px;margin-top:6px}
.cite{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;line-height:1.85;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:12px;word-break:break-word}
.cite b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:600}
</style>
</head>
<body class="mudavym" data-ground="paper">
<div class="banner">
  <span>Sketch 104 &middot; direction A &middot; first evidence &mdash; example data, not a tenant</span>
  <span class="ground" role="group" aria-label="Ground">
    <button type="button" data-g="paper" aria-pressed="true">Paper</button>
    <button type="button" data-g="charcoal" aria-pressed="false">Charcoal</button>
  </span>
</div>
<div class="wrap">

<div class="wordmark">Mudavym<span class="dot">.</span></div>
<h1>The arrival, A &mdash; the first row before the first setting<span style="color:var(--seal)">.</span></h1>
<p class="lede">One photograph at the door becomes six lines, a vendor, a price and a currency. Every register is then read from what the paper said and confirmed in place. Nothing is asked that the paper already answered; what the paper did not answer stays open, and is never defaulted.</p>
<div class="dbl"></div>

<div class="flow">
  <div class="st"><div class="n">1 &middot; /register</div><div class="t">Two doors, six fields</div><div class="c">no currency asked here</div></div>
  <div class="st"><div class="n">2 &middot; /verify-email</div><div class="t">Open it on the phone</div><div class="c">the link signs the phone in</div></div>
  <div class="st evid"><div class="n">3 &middot; the door</div><div class="t">Photograph the last invoice</div><div class="c">POST /procurement/documents</div></div>
  <div class="st evid"><div class="n">4 &middot; what the paper answered</div><div class="t">Lines, vendor, price, currency</div><div class="c">registers confirmed in place &middot; one seal</div></div>
  <div class="st"><div class="n">5 &middot; the book, opened</div><div class="t">One row, and what is still open</div><div class="c">the assistant takes the rest</div></div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 1 ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="sec">
  <div class="sec-h"><span class="sec-n">Screen 1</span><h2>/register &mdash; two doors, and a shorter founding record</h2></div>
  <div class="sec-k">Path B asks six things. The currency, the vendors, the registers, the thresholds are not on this page because the paper will answer them.</div>
  <div class="screen"><span class="cap">1440 &middot; /register</span>
    <div class="app-h"><span class="wordmark">Mudavym<span class="dot">.</span></span><span class="right">Already here? <a href="#">Sign in</a></span></div>
    <div class="paths">
      <div class="path">
        <div class="eyebrow seal">Open a house</div>
        <div class="title">Lokanta Meyhane</div>
        <div class="ask">Six things, then the door.</div>
        <div class="field"><label for="a1">Your name</label><input class="in" id="a1" value="Hasan Demir"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="a2">Email</label><input class="in" id="a2" value="hasan@lokantameyhane.example"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="a3">Password</label><input class="in" id="a3" type="password" value="passwordpassword"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="a4">The house</label><input class="in" id="a4" value="Lokanta Meyhane"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="a5">Address</label><input class="in" id="a5" value="Nevizade Sk. 12, Beyoğlu, İstanbul, Türkiye"><div class="help">Chosen from the list, so the house has a point on the map. A typed address stores no point rather than a guess.</div></div>
        <div style="margin-top:16px"><span class="btn">Open the house</span></div>
        <div class="notasked">
          <div class="eyebrow">Not asked here, on purpose</div>
          <ul style="padding-left:16px;margin:6px 0 0">
            <li><b>Currency</b> &mdash; the invoice prints it. If the paper prints none, the address&rsquo;s country stands in, stated as a default.</li>
            <li><b>Vendors and their terms</b> &mdash; the invoice names one and prints its payment terms.</li>
            <li><b>What the house pours</b> &mdash; read from the lines the paper created, then confirmed.</li>
            <li><b>Thresholds, notifications</b> &mdash; offered after the first row, never before it.</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
        <div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">writes &rarr; POST /auth/register/restaurant (auth.controller.ts:390; register-restaurant.dto.ts:13-16). currency omitted &rarr; the row stays NULL &mdash; the path Register.tsx:985-990 already sends when nobody answered.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="vr"></div>
      <div class="path">
        <div class="eyebrow seal">Join a house</div>
        <div class="title">You were expected</div>
        <div class="ask">The code says which house and who is waiting, before the account exists.</div>
        <div class="field"><label for="b1">Invite code</label><input class="in mono" id="b1" value="K7M2P9QX" maxlength="8"></div>
        <div class="resolved">
          <div class="who">Hasan is expecting you at Lokanta Meyhane, İstanbul &mdash; as a manager.</div>
          <div class="k">resolved by GET /auth/invite/:code &middot; restaurant &middot; city &middot; inviter &middot; role</div>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label for="b2">Your name</label><input class="in" id="b2" value="Defne Aksoy"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="b3">Email</label><input class="in" id="b3" value="defne@lokantameyhane.example"></div>
        <div class="field"><label for="b4">Password</label><input class="in" id="b4" type="password" value="passwordpassword"></div>
        <div style="margin-top:16px"><span class="btn">Join Lokanta Meyhane</span></div>
        <p style="font-size:12px;margin-top:14px">A joiner arrives inside the house&rsquo;s book, not at an empty one. If the book has no rows yet, a manager is handed the same door as the owner &mdash; the first row can be Defne&rsquo;s. Staff arrive to the read-only welcome that exists today.</p>
        <div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">preview &rarr; GET /auth/invite/:code (auth.controller.ts:405; shape InviteLanding.tsx:11-20) &middot; join &rarr; POST /auth/join (auth.controller.ts:435; code, name, email) &middot; ?invite=CODE prefill Register.tsx:173-178 &middot; staff &rarr; StaffWelcome (GetStarted.tsx:234-236). Google sign-UP is not a route on this tree (oauth/google is sign-in, auth.controller.ts:113) and is not drawn as one.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 2 ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="sec">
  <div class="sec-h"><span class="sec-n">Screen 2</span><h2>/verify-email &mdash; open it on the phone you will photograph with</h2></div>
  <div class="sec-k">The verification link stores the session on whichever device opens it. So the email is the hand-off to the camera, and the screen says so.</div>
  <div class="screen"><span class="cap">1440 &middot; /verify-email</span>
    <div class="verify">
      <div>
        <div class="eyebrow seal">One more thing before the door</div>
        <div class="title">We sent a link to hasan@lokantameyhane.example</div>
        <div class="ask">Open it on the phone you will photograph with. The link signs that phone in, and the door is the next screen.</div>
        <p style="margin-top:14px">Nothing waits behind this page but the camera. There is no list to import and no settings to walk through first &mdash; the house&rsquo;s first row is whatever paper came through the door last.</p>
        <div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center"><span class="btn dry">Send it again</span><span class="help">one per minute</span></div>
        <div class="note" style="margin-top:14px">redeem &rarr; POST /auth/verify-email returns accessToken + refreshToken and the page stores them on the device that opened the link (VerifyEmail.tsx:31-37, auth.controller.ts:448) &middot; resend &rarr; POST /auth/resend-verification (:457; VerifyEmail.tsx:55-62). Today the redirect is /get-started (VerifyEmail.tsx:41-45); in this direction that page IS the door.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mail">
        <div class="from">from Mudavym &middot; to hasan@lokantameyhane.example</div>
        <div class="subj">Lokanta Meyhane &mdash; confirm your email, then photograph one invoice</div>
        <div class="body">Hasan, tap the link on your phone. It signs you in there, and the first thing you will see is a camera. Photograph the last invoice that came through the door &mdash; that is the whole set-up.<br><span class="lnk">Confirm and open the door</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 3 ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="sec">
  <div class="sec-h"><span class="sec-n">Screen 3</span><h2>The door &mdash; photograph the last invoice</h2></div>
  <div class="sec-k">/get-started becomes one question. The phone frame is the step a manager does standing up.</div>
  <div class="door">
    <div class="screen"><span class="cap">1440 &middot; /get-started &rarr; the door</span>
      <div class="app-h"><span class="wordmark">Mudavym<span class="dot">.</span></span><span class="right">Lokanta Meyhane &middot; Hasan <a href="#">Go to the book</a></span></div>
      <div class="door-main">
        <div class="eyebrow seal">The first row</div>
        <div class="title">Photograph the last invoice that came through the door</div>
        <div class="ask">Not a list, not a form. One piece of paper the house already has, and it answers most of what we would otherwise ask.</div>
        <div class="choices">
          <div class="choice first">
            <div class="h"><svg class="ico" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 8h3l2-3h6l2 3h3v11H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="3.5"/></svg>&nbsp; An invoice or delivery note</div>
            <div class="s">The lines become the house&rsquo;s first items, the header names the vendor, the total names the currency, the footer prints the payment terms. Six lines is the usual size.</div>
            <div class="r"><span class="btn">Open the camera</span> <span class="btn plain">Upload a PDF or photo</span></div>
            <div class="note" style="margin-top:10px">POST /procurement/documents, source &ldquo;photo&rdquo; &mdash; classifies, extracts lines, stores for review, writes no stock, cost or orders (documents.controller.ts:66-77, :436-443; documents.dto.ts:19,46-49). Duplicates dedupe per house (:439).</div>
          </div>
          <div class="choice">
            <div class="h">The wine list, if no paper is in yet</div>
            <div class="s">A menu photo seeds the items and the registers but names no vendor, no price and no currency. It is the second road, kept for a house whose first delivery has not arrived.</div>
            <div class="r"><span class="btn dry">Photograph the list</span></div>
            <div class="note" style="margin-top:10px">POST /menus/import, source &ldquo;scan&rdquo; (menus.controller.ts:36; menus.ts:75-83; MenuScanUpload.tsx:14-19) &mdash; the review screen that exists today follows.</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p style="margin-top:16px;font-size:12px">A house that skips both is not silent about it: the offer is recorded as a fact (&ldquo;offered on 6 September, skipped&rdquo;), and the book opens empty with the door still on its first page.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="phone"><span class="cap">390 &middot; the door, standing up</span>
      <div class="ph-h"><span class="t">The door<span style="color:var(--seal)">.</span></span><span class="mono">Defne &middot; 14:02</span></div>
      <div class="finder">
        <div class="corner c1"></div><div class="corner c2"></div><div class="corner c3"></div><div class="corner c4"></div>
        <div class="sheet">
          <div class="l t"></div><div class="l m"></div><div class="l s"></div>
          <div style="height:8px"></div>
          <div class="l"></div><div class="l"></div><div class="l"></div><div class="l"></div><div class="l"></div><div class="l"></div>

        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="ph-f">

        <div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">The reader transcribes the currency it SEES and where (&ldquo;₺ beside the grand total&rdquo;) and never infers one from the vendor&rsquo;s country (document-extractor.service.ts:105,115). The whole parse is kept as the paper&rsquo;s own reading in procurement_documents.extracted (document-intake.service.ts:309).</div>
      </div>
      <div class="doc">

          <div><div class="eyebrow seal">Invoice &middot; read, not yet entered</div><div class="v"><span class="inf">Marmara İçecek Dağıtım A.Ş.</span></div><div class="prov" style="margin-top:4px">vendor name &middot; inferred from the header &middot; not yet in your book</div></div>

        </div>

        <div class="prov" style="margin-top:6px">the double rule: this account is ruled off when the seal lands, not before &middot; the money holds the paper&rsquo;s currency once the house confirms one (invoice-currency.ts rules 1-2)</div>

        <div class="regs">
          <div class="reg" data-reg="currency">
            <div class="nm">Currency<span class="r">the house reports in</span></div>
            <div class="said">The paper prints <b>₺</b> beside the grand total.<span class="q">currencySeen &middot; asPrinted &ldquo;₺&rdquo; &middot; where &ldquo;beside the grand total&rdquo;</span></div>
            <div class="val pop-anchor"><span class="v">Record TRY as the house&rsquo;s currency</span><span class="p">inferred from MID-2026-04417 &middot; not yet confirmed</span>
              <div class="pop" id="pop" hidden>
                <span class="cap">Popover &middot; 320 &middot; anchored &middot; no dim</span>
                <div class="h">Not TRY?</div>
                <div class="opt on"><span>Turkish lira</span><span class="c">TRY &middot; on the paper</span></div>
                <div class="opt"><span>Euro</span><span class="c">EUR</span></div>

                <div class="opt"><span>Pound sterling</span><span class="c">GBP</span></div>

            <div class="act"><span class="btn yes" data-do="confirm">Confirm</span><span class="btn plain" data-pop="pop">Change</span><span class="btn plain no" data-do="skip">Not now</span><span class="btn plain chg" data-do="reset">Undo</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="note" style="padding:0 0 10px">writes &rarr; PUT /settings/currency, owner or manager, audited (settings.controller.ts:227; house-currency.service.ts rules 1-3) &middot; if the paper printed no currency: &ldquo;Defaulted from Türkiye. We will record TRY. Change it if that is wrong.&rdquo; (CurrencyStep.tsx:51-53) &middot; &ldquo;Not now&rdquo; records nothing and every money figure says &ldquo;currency not recorded&rdquo;.</div>

          <div class="reg" data-reg="vendor">
            <div class="nm">Vendor<span class="r">whom we buy from</span></div>
            <div class="said">The header names <b>Marmara İçecek Dağıtım A.Ş.</b>, and the paper is in lira.<span class="q">vendorName from the reading &middot; no provider row matches it</span></div>
            <div class="val"><span class="v">Add Marmara İçecek as a vendor; usually invoices in TRY</span><span class="p">name inferred from the header &middot; usual currency inferred from this one paper</span></div>
            <div class="act"><span class="btn yes" data-do="confirm">Add the vendor</span><span class="btn plain no" data-do="skip">Not now</span><span class="btn plain chg" data-do="reset">Undo</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="note" style="padding:0 0 10px">writes &rarr; POST /providers with the name pre-filled from the upload&rsquo;s returned parse (providers.controller.ts:201; documents.controller.ts:503-509) then PATCH /providers/:id/usual-currency (:282, managers and owners). <b>Gap:</b> nothing matches a paper&rsquo;s vendor name to a provider row; the name lives only in the extracted jsonb, so the person is the match.</div>

          <div class="reg" data-reg="terms">
            <div class="nm">Vendor terms<span class="r">Marmara İçecek</span></div>
            <div class="said">The footer prints <b>&ldquo;Vade: 30 gün&rdquo;</b>. It says nothing about delivery days, lead time, cut-off or a minimum.<span class="q">one invoice is one sighting, not a pattern &mdash; a Tuesday delivery is not &ldquo;delivers Tuesdays&rdquo;</span></div>
            <div class="val"><span class="v">Payment terms: Net 30 &mdash; typed by you, from the footer</span><span class="p">delivery days &middot; lead time &middot; cut-off &middot; minimum: not yet answered</span></div>
            <div class="act"><span class="btn yes" data-do="confirm">Record Net 30</span><span class="btn plain no" data-do="skip">Not now</span><span class="btn plain chg" data-do="reset">Undo</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="note" style="padding:0 0 10px">writes &rarr; PUT /vendor-terms/:providerId with paymentTerms only; the other four columns stay NULL, and NULL means nobody said (vendor-terms.controller.ts:71; vendor-terms.dto.ts:27-90). <b>Not built:</b> the reader&rsquo;s contract has no payment-terms field (document-extractor.service.ts:98-120), so this value is typed, not inferred, and its provenance says so.</div>

          <div class="reg" data-reg="pours">
            <div class="nm">What this house pours<span class="r">cellar registers</span></div>
            <div class="said">Six lines name three wines, a rakı, a pilsner and a soda.<span class="q">read over the house items the paper creates &mdash; runs after the seal, not before it</span></div>
            <div class="val"><span class="v">Carries wines, spirits, beer, soft drinks (so non-alcoholic too)</span><span class="p">whiskey &middot; cocktails: not yet answered &mdash; the paper did not say</span></div>
            <div class="act"><span class="btn yes" data-do="confirm">Confirm</span><span class="btn plain">Edit</span><span class="btn plain no" data-do="skip">Confirm later</span><span class="btn plain chg" data-do="reset">Undo</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="note" style="padding:0 0 10px">writes &rarr; PUT /cellar/:restaurantId/registers, source &ldquo;confirmed&rdquo; (cellar.controller.ts:115; cellar-registers.dto.ts:31-60). The inference reads inventory kinds and names, menu items and cocktails (cellar-registers.ts:332-343), never a document&rsquo;s lines &mdash; so it can only speak once the six lines are house items. A register with no rows behind it says &ldquo;not yet answered&rdquo;, never &ldquo;no&rdquo; (:372-378).</div>

          <div class="reg" data-reg="threshold">
            <div class="nm">Low-stock line<span class="r">one number to start</span></div>
            <div class="said">The paper says how the house buys: by the dozen.<span class="q">offered, not inferred &mdash; the default is stated as a default</span></div>
            <div class="val"><span class="v">Flag a wine below 6 bottles</span><span class="p">default &middot; nobody has said this yet &middot; tune per wine later</span></div>
            <div class="act"><span class="btn yes" data-do="confirm">Keep 6</span><span class="btn plain no" data-do="skip">Not now</span><span class="btn plain chg" data-do="reset">Undo</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="note" style="padding:0 0 10px">writes &rarr; PATCH /onboarding/threshold (menus.controller.ts:91-99). &ldquo;Not now&rdquo; today writes nothing (ThresholdStep.tsx:80-81); here it writes the skip row below. <b>Not built:</b> the skip writer (get-started.md:193; sketch 101 onboarding-step.html:235-245).</div>

          <div class="audit" id="audit">
INSERT INTO <span class="vv">public.system_audit_log</span> &nbsp;<span style="color:var(--ink-3)">-- not built: the skip writer of get-started.md &sect;13.7</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kk">actor_id</span> &rarr; <span class="vv">public.users.user_id from the JWT</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kk">action</span> &rarr; <span class="vv">'configuration_step_skipped'</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kk">changes</span> &rarr; <span class="vv">{ register: '<span id="audit-reg">threshold</span>', offered: ['<span id="audit-off">threshold</span>'], answered: [], evidence: 'MID-2026-04417' }</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kk">reason</span> &rarr; <span class="vv">'offered on 6 September at the first row, skipped'</span><br>
<span style="color:var(--ink-3)">-- no setting is changed by this row. It records that the paper offered, and a person declined.</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="commit">
          <div>
            <div class="say">Enter it in the book.</div>
            <div class="sub">This is the one commitment of the arrival: the six lines become the house&rsquo;s first items, the paper becomes its first document, and the delivery it records becomes the first event. Everything above is a record and reverses in one click; this does not.</div>
            <ul class="writes">
              <li><b>items</b> &rarr; POST /inventory/:restaurantId/items/bulk, one line each, wineDraft resolved or created Provisional (inventory.controller.ts:77-83)</li>
              <li><b>the paper</b> &rarr; POST /procurement/documents/:id/verify &mdash; &ldquo;the transcription is faithful&rdquo;; accepts no charge, moves no stock (documents.controller.ts:1136-1142)</li>
              <li><b>the event</b> &rarr; POST /procurement/deliveries, no orderId &rarr; permanently UNORDERED, the invoice attached with its role (deliveries.controller.ts:80-99, :143-163)</li>
              <li><b>stock</b> &rarr; on origin/main fed2b7ca the door count books provisional lots against the delivery when each line names its item (documents.controller.ts:307,415-429 there; deliveries.dto.ts:148-154); on this tree (5443a0b8) the same route books nothing. Both are said, neither is assumed.</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
          <div>
            <button type="button" class="hold" id="hold" aria-label="Press and hold to enter the row">
              <span class="fill"></span>
              <span class="lbl"><span>Hold to enter the row</span><span class="k">pour 620 ms</span></span>
            </button>
            <div class="early" id="early">Released early &mdash; nothing was entered.</div>
            <div class="stamped" id="stamped">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="48" height="48" style="color:var(--seal)" aria-hidden="true">
                <path fill="currentColor" d="M50 6c9 0 14 6 22 8s16-2 21 5-1 15 1 23 8 13 5 21-13 8-18 14-6 15-14 17-14-4-22-4-14 6-22 4-9-11-14-17-15-6-18-14 3-13 5-21-3-16 1-23 13-3 21-5S41 6 50 6z"/>
                <g transform="translate(50 47) scale(0.0592) translate(-241.5 -287)" fill="var(--paper-0)">

              </svg>
              <div class="rb">Marmara İçecek, 2 September, six lines, ₺23.976,00. Entered by Defne on 6 September.<span class="m">document verified &middot; delivery UNORDERED &middot; 6 items created &middot; stock: booked provisionally on main, not on this tree &middot; cost: settles at VERIFIED</span></div>
            </div>
            <div class="note" style="margin-top:10px">The seal fires once; an early release enters nothing and says so (HoldToApprove semantics, ADR 0113). Price sighting: a class-A row is written only from a verified receipt that carries a currency (own-paper-sighting.ts:282-296) &mdash; the receiving screen sends none today, so this paper&rsquo;s prices reach the document lines and not the price register.</div>
          </div>
        </div>
