<!-- ───────────────────────── 5 ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="sec">
  <div class="sec-h"><span class="sec-n">Screen 5</span><h2>The book, opened &mdash; one row, and what is still open</h2></div>
  <div class="sec-k">A checklist with state, never a percentage. Each register says how it was decided; each silence says whether it was a choice.</div>
  <div class="screen"><span class="cap">1440 &middot; the book &middot; first page</span>
    <div class="app-h"><span class="wordmark">Mudavym<span class="dot">.</span></span><span class="right">Lokanta Meyhane &middot; Defne <a href="#">Inventory</a><a href="#">Receipts</a><a href="#">Settings</a></span></div>
    <div class="book">
      <div class="ledger">
        <div class="eyebrow seal">The ledger &middot; 1 row</div>
        <div class="title" style="font-size:24px">The house&rsquo;s book begins on 2 September</div>
        <div class="row1">
          <span class="d">2 Sep</span>
          <span class="w">Marmara İçecek Dağıtım &middot; invoice MID-2026-04417<small>6 lines &middot; 36 bottles and 5 cases &middot; entered 6 Sep by Defne &middot; unordered</small></span>
          <span class="a">₺23.976,00</span>
        </div>
        <div class="stock">On the shelf: 36 bottles of wine and rakı, 48 pilsner, 72 soda &mdash; <b>provisionally</b>, with no cost yet. Cost posts when a person verifies the delivery. <span class="mono">(origin/main; on this tree the row exists and the shelf does not move)</span></div>
        <div class="open-q">
          <div class="eyebrow">What the paper did not answer</div>
          <div class="assist">
            <div>
              <div class="h">Talk the rest through &mdash; by voice or by typing</div>
              <div class="s">The assistant has read this paper. It can propose the four vendor terms the footer did not print, the two registers the lines did not name, and the quiet hours &mdash; each with a reason and where it came from &mdash; and one seal applies them item by item.</div>
              <div class="gap">not built &mdash; would need config.propose_batch (ADR 0113 rule 5, :298-313); the seal in a first-party client is the only caller of apply. Drawn as an offer, not as a working control.</div>
            </div>
            <span class="btn off">Talk it through</span>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:16px" class="eyebrow">Notifications &middot; nine producers, defaults kept &middot; offered, not inferred</div>
          <div class="nine">
            <div class="pr"><span>Goal reached</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Ceiling held</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Delivery recorded</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Invoice confirmed</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Sale record</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Market price</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Added tool</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Grant suspended</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
            <div class="pr"><span>Experiment ended</span><span class="k">kept</span></div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin-top:10px"><span class="btn dry">Change which ones ring</span><span class="btn plain">Keep the defaults</span><span class="help">&ldquo;Keep&rdquo; is recorded as offered on 6 September, kept &mdash; not as silence.</span></div>
          <div class="note" style="margin-top:8px">writes &rarr; PATCH /notifications/preferences (notifications.controller.ts:237). <b>Gap:</b> the DTO holds five categories, quiet hours, low-stock and two modes (notifications.dto.ts:193-218, 251-300) &mdash; a switch per producer is not built, so the nine are offered and written as the categories they fall into.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="states">
        <div class="eyebrow">Registers &middot; how each was decided</div>
        <div class="state ruled"><span class="n">Currency</span><span class="v ink">TRY &middot; from MID-2026-04417 &middot; confirmed 6 Sep by Defne</span></div>
        <div class="state ruled"><span class="n">Vendor<small>Marmara İçecek Dağıtım</small></span><span class="v ink">added 6 Sep &middot; usually TRY &middot; from this paper</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Payment terms</span><span class="v ink">Net 30 &middot; typed 6 Sep by Defne, from the footer</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Delivery days &middot; lead time &middot; cut-off &middot; minimum</span><span class="v">not yet answered &middot; the paper did not say</span></div>
        <div class="state ruled"><span class="n">What we pour</span><span class="v ink">wines &middot; spirits &middot; beer &middot; soft drinks &middot; confirmed 6 Sep</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Whiskey &middot; cocktails</span><span class="v">not yet answered &middot; no rows to read</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Low-stock line</span><span class="v" id="st-threshold">offered 6 Sep &middot; skipped &middot; nothing changed</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Notifications</span><span class="v">defaults &middot; offered 6 Sep &middot; kept</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">The assistant</span><span class="v">offered 6 Sep &middot; not built on this tree</span></div>
        <div class="state"><span class="n">Google sign-up</span><span class="v">not a route &middot; not drawn</span></div>
        <div class="note" style="margin-top:12px">Three silences, three sentences: &ldquo;confirmed by&rdquo;, &ldquo;offered and skipped&rdquo;, &ldquo;never asked&rdquo;. The double rule under a register means the house has ruled it off; a single rule means a subtotal &mdash; something is still open beneath it.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── roads not taken ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="sec">
  <div class="sec-h"><span class="sec-n">Inside A</span><h2>Two roads not taken</h2></div>
  <div class="two">
    <div class="panel">
      <div class="h">Road 1 &middot; keep the currency question at /register</div>
      <div class="big">Ask it twice: once from the address, once from the paper.</div>
      <p>Safer for a house whose first invoice prints no currency. Rejected because it puts a setting before the evidence &mdash; the one thing this direction refuses &mdash; and because the paper&rsquo;s sighting is stronger evidence than a country table. The address stays as the stated fallback when the paper is mute.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="panel">
      <div class="h">Road 2 &middot; a seal per register</div>
      <div class="big">Six ceremonies instead of one.</div>
      <p>Every confirmed value would carry its own wax. Rejected because the wax is rationed to the real commitment, and a currency, a vendor name and a threshold are records that reverse in one click. The row entering the book is the only thing here that does not.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
(function(){
  var body=document.body;
  var reduced=window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  /* ground */
  var gb=document.querySelectorAll('.ground button');
  gb.forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){
    body.setAttribute('data-ground',b.getAttribute('data-g'));
    gb.forEach(function(x){x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',x===b?'true':'false')});
  })});

  /* tally: the total counts up once (overdamped, 840ms) */
  var t=document.getElementById('tally');
  function fmt(n){var s=Math.round(n).toString();var out='';while(s.length>3){out='.'+s.slice(-3)+out;s=s.slice(0,-3)}return s+out+',00'}
  var to=+t.getAttribute('data-to'),ran=false;
  function runTally(){
    if(ran)return;ran=true;
    if(reduced){t.textContent=fmt(to);return}
    var st=null;
    function step(ts){if(!st)st=ts;var p=Math.min(1,(ts-st)/840);var e=1-Math.pow(1-p,3);t.textContent=fmt(to*e);if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(step)}
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }
  if('IntersectionObserver' in window){new IntersectionObserver(function(en){en.forEach(function(x){if(x.isIntersecting)runTally()})},{threshold:.4}).observe(t)}else{runTally()}

  /* registers: confirm / skip / undo — a record, reversible in one click */
  var audit=document.getElementById('audit');
  document.querySelectorAll('.reg[data-reg]').forEach(function(reg){
    var v=reg.querySelector('.val .v'),p=reg.querySelector('.val .p');
    var orig={v:v.textContent,p:p.textContent};
    reg.querySelectorAll('[data-do]').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){
      var d=b.getAttribute('data-do');
      reg.classList.remove('confirmed','skipped');
      if(d==='confirm'){reg.classList.add('confirmed');p.textContent=(reg.getAttribute('data-reg')==='terms'?'typed':'inferred from MID-2026-04417')+' · confirmed by Defne · 6 September';}
      else if(d==='skip'){reg.classList.add('skipped');v.textContent='not yet answered';p.textContent='offered on 6 September, skipped · nothing changed';
        document.getElementById('audit-reg').textContent=reg.getAttribute('data-reg');document.getElementById('audit-off').textContent=reg.getAttribute('data-reg');audit.classList.add('on');}
      else{v.textContent=orig.v;p.textContent=orig.p;}
      v.style.animation='none';void v.offsetWidth;v.style.animation='';
    })});
  });

  /* the popover: anchored, no dim, close is words */
  var pop=document.getElementById('pop');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-pop]').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){pop.hidden=!pop.hidden})});
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-close]').forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){pop.hidden=true})});
  pop.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(function(o){o.addEventListener('click',function(){pop.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('on')});o.classList.add('on');
    var code=o.querySelector('.c').textContent.split(' ')[0];var reg=document.querySelector('[data-reg="currency"]');reg.querySelector('.val .v').textContent='Record '+code+' as the house’s currency';reg.querySelector('.val .p').textContent=(code==='TRY'?'inferred from MID-2026-04417':'chosen by Defne · the paper printed ₺')+' · not yet confirmed';pop.hidden=true;})});

  /* the seal: press and hold; the pour fills 620ms; the wax lands once */
  var hold=document.getElementById('hold'),early=document.getElementById('early'),stamped=document.getElementById('stamped'),timer=null,done=false;
  function start(e){if(done)return;e.preventDefault();early.classList.remove('on');hold.classList.add('pouring');
    timer=setTimeout(function(){done=true;hold.classList.remove('pouring');hold.classList.add('done');hold.querySelector('.lbl span').textContent='Entered';hold.querySelector('.k').textContent='stamp 360 ms';stamped.classList.add('on');
      },reduced?0:620)}
  function stop(){if(done)return;if(timer){clearTimeout(timer);timer=null;if(hold.classList.contains('pouring')){early.classList.add('on')}}hold.classList.remove('pouring')}
  hold.addEventListener('pointerdown',start);hold.addEventListener('pointerup',stop);hold.addEventListener('pointerleave',stop);hold.addEventListener('pointercancel',stop);
  hold.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if((e.key===' '||e.key==='Enter')&&!e.repeat)start(e)});
  hold.addEventListener('keyup',function(e){if(e.key===' '||e.key==='Enter')stop()});
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">

<title>104 &middot; Direction B &middot; The interview</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:ital,opsz,wght@0,9..144,400;0,9..144,600;1,9..144,400&family=DM+Sans:wght@400;500;600&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
.mudavym{
  --seal:#1A5E6B; --seal-deep:#14515C; --seal-tint:rgba(26,94,107,.10); --seal-ring:rgba(26,94,107,.32);
  --paper-0:#FAF7F1; --paper-1:#F3EFE6; --paper-2:#EAE4D8;
  --ink-1:#211C16; --ink-2:#4F473C; --ink-3:#7C7365; --ink-4:#665D50;
  color-scheme:light;
}
.mudavym[data-ground="charcoal"]{
  --seal:#5FB0BC; --seal-deep:#7DC3CD; --seal-tint:rgba(95,176,188,.14); --seal-ring:rgba(95,176,188,.38);
  --paper-0:#15130F; --paper-1:#1D1813; --paper-2:#262019;
  --ink-1:#EFE7D9; --ink-2:#C0B6A5; --ink-3:#8E8576; --ink-4:#ABA294;
  color-scheme:dark;
}
:root{--serif:"Fraunces",Georgia,serif;--sans:"DM Sans",system-ui,sans-serif;--mono:"JetBrains Mono",ui-monospace,monospace}

/* motion, named from the house tokens */
:root{
  --settle:cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1); --settle-t:320ms;
  --ink-t:160ms;
  --tuck:cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1); --tuck-t:300ms;
  --turn:cubic-bezier(.32,.72,0,1); --turn-t:420ms;
  --pour-t:620ms;
  --stamp:cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1); --stamp-t:360ms;
  --tally-t:840ms;
}
@keyframes settle{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(7px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}
@keyframes stamp{0%{transform:scale(.55) rotate(-6deg);opacity:0}62%{transform:scale(1.07) rotate(1deg);opacity:1}100%{transform:scale(1) rotate(0)}}
@keyframes turn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateX(18px)}to{opacity:1;transform:none}}
.arrive{animation:settle var(--settle-t) var(--settle) both}
.turned{animation:turn var(--turn-t) var(--turn) both}

*{box-sizing:border-box}
html{background:var(--paper-1)}
body{margin:0;min-width:1440px;background:var(--paper-1);color:var(--ink-1);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;transition:background var(--ink-t) linear,color var(--ink-t) linear}
button{font:inherit;color:inherit;background:none;border:0;padding:0;cursor:pointer}
button:focus-visible,[tabindex]:focus-visible,input:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--seal);outline-offset:2px}
code,.mono{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-3)}
b{font-weight:600}

.top{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--ink-1);color:var(--paper-0);padding:8px 40px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase}
.ground{display:flex;gap:16px}
.ground button{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);padding:2px 0;border-bottom:1px solid transparent;transition:color var(--ink-t) linear}
.ground button[aria-pressed="true"]{color:var(--paper-0);border-bottom-color:var(--paper-0)}

.wrap{width:1440px;margin:0 auto;padding:30px 40px 60px}
.wordmark{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:600;font-size:14px;letter-spacing:.03em}
.wordmark i{font-style:normal;color:var(--seal)}
h1{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:34px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.018em;line-height:1.08;margin:6px 0 0}
h1 i{font-style:normal;color:var(--seal)}
.lede{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:16px;color:var(--ink-2);margin:8px 0 0;max-width:960px}
.dbl{height:5px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);margin:20px 0 26px}
.rule{height:1px;background:var(--paper-2);margin:26px 0}

.fh{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:16px;margin:34px 0 8px}
.fh .n{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);white-space:nowrap}
.fh h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:23px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.012em;margin:0}
.fk{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin:0 0 12px}
.fk b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:400}

.screen{background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);width:1360px}
.chrome{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:12px 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.chrome .r{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.stage{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 440px}
.stage.wide{grid-template-columns:1fr 560px}
.talk{padding:22px 28px 26px;min-width:0}
.talk-h{display:flex;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;padding-bottom:12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);margin-bottom:6px}
.talk-h .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:19px;font-weight:600}
.talk-h .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}

.turn{display:grid;grid-template-columns:86px 1fr;gap:14px;padding:14px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.turn:last-child{border-bottom:0}
.who{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);padding-top:4px}
.who small{display:block;margin-top:3px;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.08em;font-size:8.5px;text-transform:none}
.ask{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:16.5px;line-height:1.42;color:var(--ink-1);margin:0}
.ask.q{font-weight:600}
.said{font-family:var(--sans);font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--ink-1);margin:0}
.said q{quotes:"\201C" "\201D"}
.sub{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-2);margin:6px 0 0;line-height:1.5}
.note{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.04em;line-height:1.7;color:var(--ink-3);margin:8px 0 0;padding-left:10px;border-left:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.moved{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:8px}
.moved .m{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-2);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:3px 7px}
.moved .m.g{color:var(--ink-3);border-style:dashed;font-style:italic}

.answers{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink-2);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-0);padding:7px 12px;transition:border-color var(--ink-t) linear,color var(--ink-t) linear}
.chip:hover{border-color:var(--ink-3);color:var(--ink-1)}
.chip[aria-pressed="true"]{color:var(--ink-1);border-color:var(--ink-1)}
.chip.seal{background:var(--seal);color:var(--paper-0);border-color:var(--seal)}
.chip.seal:hover{background:var(--seal-deep);border-color:var(--seal-deep);color:var(--paper-0)}
.chip.plain{border-color:transparent;padding-left:4px;padding-right:4px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.chip.plain:hover{color:var(--ink-1)}

.fields{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px 14px;margin-top:10px;max-width:640px}
.fields.one{grid-template-columns:1fr}
.f label{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-bottom:4px}
.f .v{border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-3);padding:6px 0;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-1)}
.f .v.empty{color:var(--ink-3)}
.f .v.code{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:22px;letter-spacing:.28em;font-weight:600}
.f .h{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px}

.card{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);background:var(--paper-1);padding:14px 16px;margin-top:10px;max-width:640px}
.card .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:18px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.3}
.card .m{display:flex;gap:18px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.card .m div{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.card .m div b{display:block;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:500;margin-top:2px}

.cta{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:var(--seal);color:var(--paper-0);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;padding:11px 18px;letter-spacing:.01em;transition:background var(--ink-t) linear}
.cta:hover{background:var(--seal-deep)}
.cta.dry{background:transparent;color:var(--ink-1);border:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.cta.dry:hover{background:var(--paper-1)}
.btnrow{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center;margin-top:16px}

/* the ledger and the proposal */
.ledger{padding:20px 22px 22px;background:var(--paper-1);border-left:1px solid var(--paper-2);min-width:0}
.ledger-h{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);padding-bottom:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.ledger-h b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:400}
.grp{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-2);margin:16px 0 4px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px}
.grp span{color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:none;font-style:italic;text-align:right}
.lrow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:4px 12px;padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);align-items:start}
.lrow .f{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:500}
.lrow .f small{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:2px}
.lrow .v{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-1);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}
.lrow .v.g{color:var(--ink-3);font-style:italic}
.lrow .v.off{color:var(--ink-3);text-decoration:line-through}
.lrow .why{grid-column:1/-1;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.5}
.lrow .why q{quotes:"\201C" "\201D"}
.lrow .pv{grid-column:1/-1;display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-top:2px}
.lrow .act{justify-self:end}
.lrow.dropped .f,.lrow.dropped .v,.lrow.dropped .why{color:var(--ink-3)}
.lrow.dropped .v{text-decoration:line-through}
.lrow.told{background:var(--paper-0);margin:6px -10px 0;padding:9px 10px;border:1px dashed var(--paper-2)}
.pvc{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;padding:2px 6px;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);color:var(--ink-3);background:var(--paper-0)}
.pvc.typed,.pvc.spoken,.pvc.confirmed,.pvc.chosen,.pvc.stated{color:var(--ink-1);border-color:var(--ink-3)}
.pvc.spoken::before{content:"";width:5px;height:8px;border:1px solid currentColor;border-radius:3px}
.pvc.inferred{border-style:dashed;font-style:italic}
.pvc.defaulted{border-style:dotted}
.pvc.skipped,.pvc.unanswered,.pvc.told{color:var(--ink-3)}
.pvc.nb{border-color:var(--ink-3);border-style:double;color:var(--ink-2)}
.tog{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase}
.tog button{padding:3px 7px;color:var(--ink-3);transition:color var(--ink-t) linear,background var(--ink-t) linear}
.tog button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--ink-1);color:var(--paper-0)}
.tally{margin-top:16px;padding-top:12px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.tally .n{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:30px;font-weight:600;line-height:1;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.tally .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px;line-height:1.7}
.tally .k b{color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:400}

/* the seal */
.sealbar{margin-top:14px;padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.sealbar .warn{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.5;margin:0 0 10px}
.hold{position:relative;height:46px;border:1px solid var(--seal-ring);background:var(--paper-0);overflow:hidden;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none;touch-action:none;cursor:pointer;width:100%;text-align:left}
.hold .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;width:0;background:var(--seal-tint)}
.hold.pouring .fill{width:100%;transition:width var(--pour-t) linear}
.hold.retreat .fill{width:0;transition:width var(--tuck-t) var(--tuck)}
.hold .lbl{position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;height:100%;padding:0 14px;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--seal)}
.hold .disc{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;border:1.5px solid var(--seal)}
.hold.done{border-color:var(--seal);background:var(--seal-tint)}
.holdnote{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:6px;min-height:14px}
.wax{display:none;margin:10px auto 0;width:48px;height:48px}
.wax.on{display:block;animation:stamp var(--stamp-t) var(--stamp) both}
.readback{display:none;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-1);text-align:center;margin-top:8px;line-height:1.4}
.readback.on{display:block}

/* composer */
.composer{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;gap:10px;align-items:center;margin-top:16px;padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.mic{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;border:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding:9px 12px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink-1)}
.mic i{width:8px;height:13px;border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:4px;display:inline-block}
.composer .in{border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-3);padding:8px 0;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-3)}
.composer .send{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-1);padding:8px 0}
.composer .note{grid-column:1/-1;margin-top:2px}

/* overlays */
.pop{position:absolute;left:0;top:calc(100% + 8px);width:320px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--ink-3);padding:12px 14px 10px;z-index:3;animation:settle var(--settle-t) var(--settle) both}
.pop .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:15px;font-weight:600;margin:0 0 8px}
.pop .o{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:7px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--paper-2);font-size:13px}
.pop .o.cur{font-weight:600}
.pop .o small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.pop .close{display:block;margin-top:8px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.pop .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:8px;padding-top:6px;border-top:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.dim{position:relative;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);width:1360px;height:520px;overflow:hidden}
.dim .under{padding:22px 28px;opacity:.38;filter:saturate(0)}
.dim .scrim{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--ink-1);opacity:.42}
.mudavym[data-ground="charcoal"] .dim .scrim{background:var(--paper-0);opacity:.64}
.panel{position:absolute;left:50%;top:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:620px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:22px 26px 18px;z-index:2}
.panel .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:20px;font-weight:600;margin:0}
.panel p{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.55;margin:10px 0 0}
.panel .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:12px;line-height:1.8}

/* receipt */
.receipt{width:1360px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.receipt-h{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;padding:16px 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.receipt-h .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:20px;font-weight:600}
.receipt-h .id{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
.rrow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:120px 300px 1fr;gap:16px;padding:11px 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);align-items:start}
.rrow .st{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-1);padding-top:2px}
.rrow .st.r{color:var(--ink-1);text-decoration:underline;text-decoration-style:double;text-underline-offset:3px}
.rrow .st.n{color:var(--ink-3)}
.rrow .f{font-size:13px;font-weight:500}
.rrow .f small{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:2px}
.rrow .d{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.5}
.receipt-f{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:20px;align-items:center;padding:16px 24px;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1)}
.receipt-f p{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--ink-2);margin:0;line-height:1.5}
.ruled{border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);position:relative;padding-bottom:3px}
.ruled::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:-4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1)}

/* phone */
.phone{width:390px;background:var(--paper-0);border:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding:0 0 18px;position:relative}
.phone .bar{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 16px 8px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2)}
.phone .body{padding:14px 16px}
.phone .ask{font-size:19px;font-weight:600;line-height:1.3}
.phone .sub{margin-top:6px}
.regs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin-top:12px}
.reg{border:1px solid var(--paper-2);padding:9px 10px;min-height:60px}
.reg .n{font-size:13.5px;font-weight:500}
.reg .s{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px;line-height:1.5}
.reg.on{border-color:var(--ink-1)}
.reg.inf{border-style:dashed}
.reg.inf .n{color:var(--ink-3);font-style:italic}
.phone .live{margin-top:12px;border-left:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-left:10px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45}
.phone .live small{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-bottom:3px}
.phone .micbig{margin-top:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:10px;border:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding:13px;font-weight:600;font-size:14px}
.phone .micbig i{width:9px;height:15px;border:1.5px solid currentColor;border-radius:5px;display:inline-block}
.phone .drawer{margin:14px 16px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-top:10px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
.phone .drawer .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);line-height:1.6}
.phone .drawer .k b{color:var(--ink-1);font-weight:400}

.twoup{display:grid;grid-template-columns:390px 1fr;gap:34px;align-items:start}
.legend{width:1360px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:26px}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12.5px}
th{text-align:left;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.11em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:400;padding:0 12px 7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);vertical-align:bottom}
td{padding:8px 12px 8px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--paper-2);vertical-align:top;line-height:1.45;color:var(--ink-2)}
td b{color:var(--ink-1)}
.cite{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10px;line-height:1.85;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:14px;word-break:break-word;width:1360px}
.aside{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.55;max-width:900px;margin:10px 0 0}
.ctl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-1);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink-1);padding-bottom:1px}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){
  *{animation-duration:1ms!important;transition-duration:1ms!important}
  .arrive,.turned,.wax.on,.pop{animation:none}
}
</style>
</head>
<body class="mudavym" data-ground="paper">
<div class="top">
  <div>Sketch 104 &middot; Direction B &middot; The interview &mdash; example data, not a tenant</div>
  <div class="ground" role="group" aria-label="Ground">
    <button type="button" data-ground="paper" aria-pressed="true">Paper</button>
    <button type="button" data-ground="charcoal" aria-pressed="false">Charcoal</button>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="wrap">
<div class="wordmark">Mudavym<i>.</i></div>
<h1>Direction B &mdash; The interview<i>.</i></h1>
<p class="lede">The house asks and the owner answers, by voice or by typing. Every sentence lands on the right as rows, each with its reason and where it came from. Nothing is written until one seal applies the lot, item by item &mdash; and a skip is a row too.</p>
<div class="dbl"></div>
<p class="aside">Sketch 101 drawn out to the whole arrival. Seven frames, in the order the owner meets them: the door, the joiner&rsquo;s first screen, opening a house, the letter, the interview with its proposal beside it, the receipt, and the one question that needs a panel. Then the step a manager does standing up, at phone width. Controls that would write nowhere today say so in mono on the screen.</p>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 01 THE DOOR ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">01 &middot; /register &middot; join by invite</span><h2>The door asks one question, and the code answers most of the rest.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">the same voice as the interview, typed only &mdash; there is no house to read yet, so there is no assistant yet &middot; <b>Register.tsx:24-26, 173-175, 192-213, 344-517</b></p>
<div class="screen">
  <div class="chrome"><div class="wordmark">Mudavym<i>.</i></div><div class="r">Arriving &middot; nothing written</div></div>
  <div class="stage">
    <div class="talk">
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">Were you invited, or are you opening a house?</p>
          <div class="answers">
            <button type="button" class="chip" aria-pressed="true">I was invited</button>
            <button type="button" class="chip" aria-pressed="false">I am opening a house</button>
          </div>
          <p class="note">?invite=CODE lands here with the code already in the field &middot; Register.tsx:173-175</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask">Then the code from the letter. Eight characters.</p>
          <div class="fields one" style="max-width:320px">
            <div class="f"><label>Invite code</label><div class="v code">LM7K4Q2A</div><div class="h">checked as you type, once all eight are in &middot; Register.tsx:192-213</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">Hasan is expecting you at Lokanta Meyhane &mdash; as a manager.</p>
          <div class="card">
            <div class="t">Lokanta Meyhane</div>
            <div class="m">
              <div>Where<b>Kad&#305;k&ouml;y, &#304;stanbul</b></div>
              <div>Invited by<b>Hasan Demir</b></div>
              <div>Your role<b>Manager</b></div>
              <div>The code lasts<b>7 more days</b></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p class="note">read from the code before any account exists &middot; GET /auth/invite/:code is public by decision (auth.controller.ts:405) &middot; returns restaurant, city, inviter, role (Register.tsx:28-36)</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask">Three things from you, and you are in.</p>
          <div class="fields">
            <div class="f"><label>Your name</label><div class="v">Defne Aksoy</div></div>
            <div class="f"><label>Email</label><div class="v">defne@lokantameyhane.com</div><div class="h">free &middot; GET /auth/check-email (auth.controller.ts:490)</div></div>
            <div class="f"><label>Password</label><div class="v">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</div><div class="h">eight or more &middot; Register.tsx:524-527</div></div>
          </div>
          <div class="btnrow">
            <span class="cta">Join Lokanta Meyhane</span>
            <span class="mono">POST /auth/join (auth.controller.ts:435) &middot; no letter to open: an invited joiner goes straight to the house (Register.tsx:531-532)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ledger">
      <div class="ledger-h"><span>What I have</span><b>from the code &middot; from you</b></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">House<small>from the code</small></div><div class="v">Lokanta Meyhane</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc stated">read</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Invited by<small>from the code</small></div><div class="v">Hasan Demir</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc stated">read</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Your role<small>set by Hasan when he made the code</small></div><div class="v">manager</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc stated">read</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">You<small>account</small></div><div class="v">Defne Aksoy</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc typed">typed</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Email<small>account</small></div><div class="v">defne@&hellip;</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc typed">typed</span></div></div>
      <div class="tally"><div class="n">5</div><div class="k">facts on the table &middot; 0 written<br><b>joining writes one row: your membership of the house</b></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 01b THE JOINER'S FIRST SCREEN ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">01b &middot; the joiner&rsquo;s first screen</span><h2>Defne arrives to the house as Hasan left it &mdash; as if she had signed in.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">reads only &middot; GET /cellar/:rid/registers (cellar.controller.ts:40) &middot; GET /vendor-terms (vendor-terms.controller.ts:44) &middot; GET /settings/currency (settings.controller.ts:208) &middot; a manager may answer what is open (vendor-terms.controller.ts:30-35: deliberately not owner-only); staff would see the read-only welcome (GetStarted.tsx:234-236)</p>
<div class="screen">
  <div class="chrome"><div class="wordmark">Mudavym<i>.</i></div><div class="r">Lokanta Meyhane &middot; Defne &middot; manager</div></div>
  <div class="stage">
    <div class="talk">
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">Lokanta Meyhane, as Hasan set it up on 6 September.</p>
          <p class="sub">It reports in <b>TRY</b>. It pours <b>wines, spirits, beer and soft drinks</b>. It buys from <b>Anadolu G&#305;da</b> and <b>Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira</b>, and hears about all nine things by default. Two registers are still open: Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira&rsquo;s terms, which Hasan was offered on 6 September and skipped, and the lead times, which nobody has been asked.</p>
          <div class="answers">
            <button type="button" class="chip" aria-pressed="true">Answer the two</button>
            <button type="button" class="chip">Go to the floor</button>
          </div>
          <p class="note">&ldquo;skipped&rdquo; and &ldquo;never asked&rdquo; are different sentences because the skip was recorded (frame 05) &middot; the readout renders who and when for a stated value already (vendor-terms.controller.ts:46-48)</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ledger">
      <div class="ledger-h"><span>The house&rsquo;s answers</span><b>as they stand</b></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Reports in</div><div class="v">TRY</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc stated">stated &middot; Hasan &middot; 6 Sep</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Pours</div><div class="v">wines &middot; spirits &middot; beer &middot; soft drinks</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc confirmed">confirmed &middot; Hasan</span><span class="pvc inferred">whiskey, non-alcoholic: still inferred</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Anadolu G&#305;da &middot; terms</div><div class="v">Tue, Fri &middot; Net 30</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc stated">stated &middot; Hasan</span><span class="pvc unanswered">lead time: not yet answered</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira &middot; terms</div><div class="v g">offered 6 Sep &middot; skipped</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc skipped">skipped by Hasan</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Hears about</div><div class="v">all nine</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc defaulted">defaults kept &middot; offered 6 Sep</span></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 02 OPEN A HOUSE ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">02 &middot; /register &middot; open a house</span><h2>Four questions, and the money question answered in words before it is recorded.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">typed only &middot; the account and the house are records, not a ceremony: no wax here &middot; <b>Register.tsx:725-910, 911-1384; CurrencyStep.tsx:51-57, 77</b></p>
<div class="screen">
  <div class="chrome"><div class="wordmark">Mudavym<i>.</i></div><div class="r">Arriving &middot; nothing written</div></div>
  <div class="stage">
    <div class="talk">
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">What is the house called, and who are you?</p>
          <div class="fields">
            <div class="f"><label>The house</label><div class="v">Lokanta Meyhane</div></div>
            <div class="f"><label>Your name</label><div class="v">Hasan Demir</div></div>
            <div class="f"><label>Email</label><div class="v">hasan@lokantameyhane.com</div></div>
            <div class="f"><label>Password</label><div class="v">&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;&bull;</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">Where is it?</p>
          <div class="fields one">
            <div class="f"><label>Address</label><div class="v">Moda Caddesi 41, Kad&#305;k&ouml;y, &#304;stanbul 34710, T&uuml;rkiye</div><div class="h">chosen from the list &middot; city, country, postcode and the point filled from it; a hand-edited address drops the point (Register.tsx:97-117, 1176-1210)</div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <p class="ask q">What money does this house report in?</p>
          <p class="sub">Defaulted from T&uuml;rkiye. We will record TRY. Change it if that is wrong.</p>
          <div class="answers" style="position:relative">
            <button type="button" class="chip" aria-pressed="true">Yes, TRY</button>
            <span style="position:relative;display:inline-block">
              <button type="button" class="chip" aria-pressed="false">Change it</button>
              <div class="pop" role="dialog" aria-label="Report in">
                <p class="t">Report in</p>
                <div class="o cur"><span>TRY &middot; Turkish lira</span><small>defaulted</small></div>
                <div class="o"><span>EUR &middot; Euro</span></div>
                <div class="o"><span>USD &middot; US dollar</span></div>
                <div class="o"><span>GBP &middot; Pound sterling</span></div>
                <div class="o"><span>All currencies&hellip;</span><small>CURRENCY_CODES</small></div>
                <button type="button" class="close">Not now</button>
                <div class="k">popover &middot; 320 &middot; anchored &middot; no dim &middot; ADR 0112</div>
              </div>
            </span>
            <button type="button" class="chip plain">Not yet</button>
          </div>
          <p class="sub" style="margin-top:118px">Each invoice keeps the currency its vendor billed in. Nothing is converted.</p>
          <p class="note">the sentence is CurrencyStep.tsx:51-57 verbatim &middot; &ldquo;Not yet&rdquo; sends nothing and the row stays NULL; every screen then says &ldquo;currency not recorded&rdquo; (CurrencyStep.tsx:19-21) &middot; the interview re-offers it later through PUT /settings/currency (settings.controller.ts:227)</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="turn">
        <div class="who">Mudavym</div>
        <div>
          <div class="btnrow" style="margin-top:2px">
            <span class="cta">Create the account</span>
            <span class="mono">POST /auth/register/restaurant (auth.controller.ts:390) &middot; currency sent only because you said yes (Register.tsx:985-990) &middot; then the letter (Register.tsx:997)</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="ledger">
      <div class="ledger-h"><span>What I have</span><b>written when you create the account</b></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">House</div><div class="v">Lokanta Meyhane</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc typed">typed</span></div></div>
      <div class="lrow"><div class="f">Owner</div><div class="v">Hasan Demir</div><div class="pv"><span class="pvc typed">typed</span></div></div>

        <button type="button" class="hold" id="hold" aria-label="Hold to apply the proposal"><div class="fill"></div><div class="lbl"><span class="disc"></span><span id="hold-lbl">Hold to apply thirteen rows</span></div></button>
        <div class="holdnote" id="holdnote">the fill is pour, linear, timed against your thumb &middot; an early release is tuck, and says what did not happen</div>
        <svg class="wax" id="wax" viewBox="0 0 100 100" width="48" height="48" style="color:var(--seal)" aria-hidden="true">
          <path fill="currentColor" d="M50 6c9 0 14 6 22 8s16-2 21 5-1 15 1 23 8 13 5 21-13 8-18 14-6 15-14 17-14-4-22-4-14 6-22 4-9-11-14-17-15-6-18-14 3-13 5-21-3-16 1-23 13-3 21-5S41 6 50 6z"/>
          <g transform="translate(50 47) scale(0.0592) translate(-241.5 -287)" fill="var(--paper-0)">
            <path d="M0 38H72V540H0Z"/><path d="M15 38H96L460 574H379Z"/><path d="M0 346H305L353 416H0Z"/><path d="M0 470H137V540H0Z"/><path d="M99 0H151L278.7 187.8L284.8 273Z"/><path d="M389.5 40H460.9L284.8 273L251.4 224Z"/><path d="M412 40H483V574H412Z"/>
          </g>
          <g fill="none" stroke="var(--paper-0)" stroke-linecap="square"><path d="M30 71h40" stroke-width="4.4"/><path d="M30 77h40" stroke-width="2.6"/></g>
        </svg>
        <p class="readback" id="readback">Sealed by Hasan Demir, 6 September, 11:41. Thirteen rows, one at a time. The receipt is below.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 05 THE RECEIPT ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">05 &middot; after the seal &middot; the receipt</span><h2>Item by item: written, refused, recorded, not attempted, not offered. Never one &ldquo;Done&rdquo;.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">ADR 0113 rule 4 &middot; rule 4a: revocable as one unit for seven days, and the window&rsquo;s end is on the receipt &middot; <b>not built</b>: SettingsAuditService.record never sets correlation_id or reason (settings-audit.service.ts:205-221); the reader for a batch already exists (logs-timeline.service.ts:302)</p>
<div class="receipt" id="receipt">
  <div class="receipt-h"><div class="t">Applied 6 September, 11:41 &mdash; twelve written, one refused</div><div class="id">correlation_id cfg-9b31e0 &middot; sealed by Hasan Demir &middot; reason: the utterance that produced each row</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">What we pour &middot; five answers<small>PUT /cellar/:rid/registers &middot; source: confirmed</small></div><div class="d">Wines, spirits, beer, soft drinks carried; cocktails not. Each row carries <code>confirmed_at</code> and your name. Provenance: proposed, sealed.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">What we pour &middot; two guesses<small>PUT /cellar/:rid/registers &middot; source: inferred</small></div><div class="d">Whiskey and non-alcoholic, not carried, still labelled inferred. The readout keeps saying so until somebody answers.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">Anadolu G&#305;da<small>POST /providers &middot; 201</small></div><div class="d">Vendor created. Then its terms:</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">Anadolu &middot; delivery days, payment terms<small>PUT /vendor-terms/:providerId &middot; audit: vendor_terms_changed</small></div><div class="d">Tue, Fri &middot; Net 30. Recorded as <b>stated</b>, with who and when. Lead time untouched: NULL, not yet answered.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st r">refused</div><div class="f">Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira<small>POST /providers &middot; 409</small></div><div class="d"><b>Not created, and this is the honest outcome.</b> A vendor of that name already exists on this house &mdash; created from an email on 2 September. Nothing was duplicated. The existing row is the one your skip below now names.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">Quiet hours<small>PATCH /notifications/preferences &middot; your account &middot; audit: notification_preferences_changed</small></div><div class="d">18:00 &ndash; 01:00. Held alerts arrive together in the morning.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">written</div><div class="f">Low-stock line<small>PATCH /onboarding/threshold &middot; threshold_configured: true</small></div><div class="d">6 bottles. The value did not change; its provenance did &mdash; it is now an answer with an author.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">recorded</div><div class="f">Nine producers &middot; offered, kept<small>system_audit_log &middot; no setting changed</small></div><div class="d">One row: offered on 6 September, defaults kept, reason <q>keep them all</q>. <span class="mono">not built &mdash; the audit action does not exist yet</span></div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st">recorded</div><div class="f">Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira &middot; terms &middot; skipped<small>system_audit_log &middot; no setting changed</small></div><div class="d">Offered on 6 September, skipped. The register will say so; the assistant may re-offer it truthfully. <span class="mono">not built &mdash; same action</span></div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st n">not attempted</div><div class="f">Anadolu &middot; lead time</div><div class="d">Never answered. Nothing written, nothing recorded &mdash; and the register says &ldquo;not yet answered&rdquo;, which is a different sentence from &ldquo;skipped&rdquo;.</div></div>
  <div class="rrow"><div class="st n">not offered</div><div class="f">Anadolu bills in lira &middot; per-producer switches &middot; market price drop</div><div class="d">Told on the proposal, no door today. Nothing was written and nothing could have been.</div></div>
  <div class="receipt-f">
    <p>Every written row shares one <code>correlation_id</code> and stores what it changed <b>from</b> as well as <b>to</b>. That is what makes this reversible as a unit. A row somebody changes after today is shown and skipped by the undo, never overwritten.</p>
    <div style="text-align:right"><button type="button" class="chip">Undo this setup</button><div class="mono" style="margin-top:6px">until 13 September &middot; then each value is an ordinary setting (rule 4a)</div></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 06 THE CONSENT PANEL ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">06 &middot; the one question that needs a panel</span><h2>A person&rsquo;s mailbox is asked for in plain words, in the same conversation, and recorded with the batch.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">ADR 0113 rule 6 &middot; ADR 0114 &sect;2: the house declares the attachment, each person consents for themselves &middot; centred panel, 620, dims the page, traps focus, closes with words (ADR 0112)</p>
<div class="dim">
  <div class="under">
    <div class="talk-h"><div class="t">Set up Lokanta Meyhane with me.</div><div class="k">nothing is written yet</div></div>
    <div class="turn"><div class="who">Mudavym</div><div><p class="ask">If you let me read your own mailbox I could fill Anadolu&rsquo;s lead time from their last invoice &mdash; I would ask you first, in its own panel.</p></div></div>
    <div class="turn"><div class="who">Hasan<small>typed</small></div><div><p class="said">Go on then.</p></div></div>
  </div>
  <div class="scrim"></div>
  <div class="panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="pt">
    <p class="t" id="pt">May I read your mailbox, Hasan?</p>
    <p>Only yours, and only to look for invoices from Anadolu G&#305;da and Kad&#305;k&ouml;y Bira. Whatever I find will be named on the row it fills &mdash; <em>&ldquo;lead time 2 days, from Anadolu&rsquo;s invoice of 28 August, read from Hasan&rsquo;s mailbox&rdquo;</em> &mdash; and in the batch&rsquo;s audit row, forever. Defne&rsquo;s mailbox is hers; this asks nothing of her. You can withdraw this later without touching anyone else&rsquo;s consent, and a withdrawn consent does not unmake a sealed value.</p>
    <div class="btnrow">
      <span class="cta">Yes, mine only</span>
      <button type="button" class="chip">Not now</button>
    </div>
    <div class="k">writes one row in mcp_connection_consents (ADR 0114 &sect;2) &middot; the house-declared mail connection must exist first, on /settings &middot; <b>not built</b> as a step of onboarding; the consent table exists (20260903151000_the_house_declares_a_person_consents.sql)</div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 07 THE PHONE ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">07 &middot; 390 &middot; standing at the bar with the list in hand</span><h2>The step a manager does on their feet: the photo, the guess, the word.</h2></div>
<p class="fk">one gesture per register, the seal available here or later at the desk &middot; the same rows, the same provenance</p>
<div class="twoup">
  <div class="phone">
    <div class="bar"><span>Mudavym<span style="color:var(--seal)">.</span></span><span>Lokanta &middot; 0 written</span></div>
    <div class="body">
      <p class="ask q" style="font-size:19px">What does this house pour?</p>
      <p class="sub">Read from your photo: 24 wines, 9 rak&#305; and spirits, 5 beers. Tap what is right; say what is missing.</p>
      <div class="regs">
        <div class="reg on"><div class="n">Wines</div><div class="s">inferred &middot; confirmed</div></div>
        <div class="reg on"><div class="n">Spirits</div><div class="s">inferred &middot; confirmed</div></div>
        <div class="reg on"><div class="n">Beer</div><div class="s">inferred &middot; confirmed</div></div>
        <div class="reg on"><div class="n">Soft drinks</div><div class="s">spoken &middot; carried</div></div>
        <div class="reg"><div class="n">Cocktails</div><div class="s">spoken &middot; not carried</div></div>
        <div class="reg inf"><div class="n">Whiskey</div><div class="s">inferred only &middot; tap to answer</div></div>
        <div class="reg inf"><div class="n">Non-alcoholic</div><div class="s">inferred only &middot; tap to answer</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="live"><small>heard &middot; spoken</small>Right. We do soft drinks too &mdash; ayran and cola &mdash; but no cocktails.</div>
      <div class="micbig"><i></i>Hold to talk</div>
      <p class="note" style="margin-top:8px">voice: drawn, not built (ADR 0113 Q6, Q7) &middot; the photo: POST /menus/import, scan (GetStarted.tsx:406)</p>
    </div>
    <div class="drawer">
      <div class="k"><b>7 rows</b> on the table &middot; 0 written<br>seal here, or at the desk</div>
      <span class="chip">Keep going</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div>
    <p class="aside">The phone shows the interview&rsquo;s smallest unit: one question, the guess it starts from, and the sentence that turns a guess into an answer. Confirmed registers are ink; inferred ones stay dashed and italic until tapped or spoken to; the drawer at the bottom is the proposal, folded. Nothing on this screen writes. The seal is the same hold as on the desk, and a manager who prefers to finish standing can.</p>
    <p class="aside">The two grey chips are the whole argument for provenance at phone width: a house that walks away here leaves whiskey and non-alcoholic recorded as <em>guesses</em>, and the desk shows them grey tomorrow. Today&rsquo;s step writes nothing on &ldquo;Confirm later&rdquo; (CellarRegistersOnboarding.tsx:72-79) and cannot tell tomorrow whether anyone looked.</p>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ───────────────────────── 08 LEGEND ───────────────────────── -->
<div class="fh"><span class="n">08 &middot; the marks, and the doors</span><h2>Eight ways a value can have arrived, and five routes that exist today.</h2></div>
<div class="legend">
  <table>
    <thead><tr><th style="width:38%">Mark</th><th>Means</th></tr></thead>
    <tbody>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc typed">typed</span></td><td>A person wrote it in this conversation. Ink. Reads as <b>stated</b> on every register afterwards.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc spoken">spoken</span></td><td>A person said it. Same weight as typed; the chip says how, because the record question (ADR 0113 Q7) is open.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc inferred">inferred</span></td><td>The platform read it off the house&rsquo;s own books. <b>Grey, italic, dashed</b>, until a person answers. Sealed unanswered, it is written as a guess and stays labelled one.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc confirmed">confirmed</span></td><td>An inferred value a person said yes to. The value stays; the provenance changes.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc defaulted">defaulted</span></td><td>A default stated in words before it is recorded; a person left it standing. <b>Defaulted and confirmed</b> together mean chosen.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc skipped">skipped</span></td><td>Offered, declined. One audit row, no setting changed. Reads &ldquo;offered on 6 September, skipped&rdquo;.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc unanswered">not yet answered</span></td><td>Never asked in so many words. Nothing written, nothing recorded. The honest em dash.</td></tr>
      <tr><td><span class="pvc told">cannot be recorded</span></td><td>Named on the screen, no door today. Drawn as a sentence, never as a control.</td></tr>
    </tbody>
  </table>
  <table>
    <thead><tr><th style="width:34%">Door</th><th>Route today</th><th style="width:22%">Kept where</th></tr></thead>
    <tbody>
      <tr><td><b>Reports in</b></td><td>at the door, POST /auth/register/restaurant with <code>currency</code> only when answered (Register.tsx:985-990; auth.controller.ts:390); later PUT /settings/currency (settings.controller.ts:227; house-currency.service.ts:39-44)</td><td>this house</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>What we pour</b></td><td>PUT /cellar/:rid/registers, <code>registers[{id, carried}]</code>, one <code>source</code> per write (cellar.controller.ts:115; cellar-registers.dto.ts:16-61)</td><td>this house</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>Whom we buy from</b></td><td>POST /providers (providers.controller.ts:201), then PUT /vendor-terms/:providerId (vendor-terms.controller.ts:71; vendor-terms.dto.ts:26-84)</td><td>this house</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>What we hear about</b></td><td>PATCH /notifications/preferences &mdash; channels, five families, quiet hours (notifications.controller.ts:237-258)</td><td>your account</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>The low-stock line</b></td><td>PATCH /onboarding/threshold (menus.controller.ts:91; menus.ts:132-138)</td><td>this house</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>The skip, the kept default</b></td><td>system_audit_log, <code>configuration_step_skipped</code> &mdash; <b>not built</b> (settings-audit.service.ts:86-104); no migration</td><td>this house</td></tr>
      <tr><td><b>The assistant</b></td><td>config.propose_batch &mdash; <b>not built</b> (ADR 0113 rule 5); apply is never exposed, the seal in a first-party client is its only caller</td><td>&mdash;</td></tr>
    </tbody>
  </table>
</div>

<div class="dbl" style="margin-top:36px"></div>
<div class="cite">

<script>
(function(){
  var body=document.body;
  var reduced=window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  /* ground */
  var gs=document.querySelectorAll('.ground button');
  gs.forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener('click',function(){
    body.setAttribute('data-ground',b.getAttribute('data-ground'));
    gs.forEach(function(x){x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(x===b));});
  });});

  /* keep / drop, and the tally */
  var rows=Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#proposal .lrow[data-kind="write"]'));
  var tallyN=document.getElementById('tally-n'), tallyK=document.getElementById('tally-k');
  function recount(){
    var dropped=rows.filter(function(r){return r.classList.contains('dropped');}).length;
    var writes=rows.length-dropped;
    tallyK.innerHTML='<b>'+writes+'</b> rows written through 5 doors &middot; <b>1</b> recorded as offered and kept<br><b>1</b> skipped, recorded &middot; <b>'+(1+dropped)+'</b> not yet answered, not recorded &middot; <b>3</b> cannot be recorded<br><b>'+dropped+'</b> dropped &middot; <b>0 written so far</b>';
    var lbl=document.getElementById('hold-lbl'); if(lbl&&!done) lbl.textContent='Hold to apply '+words(writes)+' row'+(writes===1?'':'s');
  }
  function words(n){var w=['zero','one','two','three','four','five','six','seven','eight','nine','ten','eleven','twelve','thirteen','fourteen'];return w[n]||String(n);}
  rows.forEach(function(r){
    var bs=r.querySelectorAll('.tog button');
    bs.forEach(function(b,i){b.addEventListener('click',function(){
      bs.forEach(function(x,j){x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(i===j));});
      r.classList.toggle('dropped',i===1);
      var why=r.querySelector('.why');
      if(i===1&&!r.querySelector('.drop-why')){var p=document.createElement('div');p.className='why drop-why';p.innerHTML='<b>Dropped by you.</b> Not written, not recorded as an answer &mdash; it stays not yet answered. Dropping is not an answer.';r.insertBefore(p,r.querySelector('.pv'));}
      if(i===0){var d=r.querySelector('.drop-why'); if(d) d.remove();}
      recount();
    });});
  });

  /* the tally count: overdamped spring, 840ms */
  function countTo(el,to){
    if(reduced){el.textContent=to;return;}
    var from=parseInt(el.textContent,10)||0, t0=performance.now();
    function step(t){var p=Math.min(1,(t-t0)/840); var e=1-Math.exp(-6*p)*(1+6*p); el.textContent=Math.round(from+(to-from)*e); if(p<1) requestAnimationFrame(step); else el.textContent=to;}
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  /* replay the turns: a sentence becomes rows */
  var stepped=Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#talk [data-step], #proposal [data-step]'));
  var replay=document.getElementById('replay'), rk=document.getElementById('replay-k');
  var timer=null;
  replay.addEventListener('click',function(){
    if(timer){clearInterval(timer);timer=null;}
    var max=0; stepped.forEach(function(el){max=Math.max(max,+el.getAttribute('data-step'));});
    stepped.forEach(function(el){ var s=+el.getAttribute('data-step'); if(s>0){ if(el.hasAttribute('hidden')||el.matches('.pvc')) el.setAttribute('hidden',''); else el.style.display='none'; } el.classList.remove('arrive'); });
    tallyN.textContent='1';
    var cur=0; rk.textContent='turn 0 of '+max;
    var onTable={0:1,1:8,3:8,5:14,7:14,9:14,11:14};
    timer=setInterval(function(){
      cur++; rk.textContent='turn '+cur+' of '+max;
      stepped.forEach(function(el){ if(+el.getAttribute('data-step')===cur){ if(el.matches('.pvc')) el.removeAttribute('hidden'); else el.style.display=''; el.classList.add('arrive'); } });
      if(cur===3){document.querySelectorAll('#proposal .lrow[data-step="1"] .v.g').forEach(function(v,i){ if(i<3){v.classList.remove('g');} });}
      var n=0; for(var k in onTable){ if(+k<=cur) n=onTable[k]; } countTo(tallyN,n);
      if(cur>=max){clearInterval(timer);timer=null; rk.textContent='';}
    }, reduced?1:760);
  });

  /* the hold: pour, tuck, stamp */
  var hold=document.getElementById('hold'), note=document.getElementById('holdnote'), wax=document.getElementById('wax'), rb=document.getElementById('readback');
  var t0=0, tm=null, armed=false, done=false, POUR=620;
  function complete(){
    done=true; hold.classList.remove('pouring'); hold.classList.add('done');
    document.getElementById('hold-lbl').textContent='Sealed';
    note.textContent='the wax lands on stamp, once; the receipt below names each row';
    wax.classList.add('on'); rb.classList.add('on');
    document.getElementById('chrome-count').textContent='13 of 14 rows written, one refused';
    tallyK.innerHTML=tallyK.innerHTML.replace('<b>0 written so far</b>','<b>sealed 11:41 &mdash; see the receipt</b>');
    document.getElementById('receipt').classList.add('turned');
  }
  function start(){ if(done) return; if(reduced){ if(!armed){armed=true;note.textContent='Press again to seal';setTimeout(function(){if(!done){armed=false;note.textContent='disarmed';}},3000);} else {complete();} return; }
    t0=performance.now(); hold.classList.remove('retreat'); void hold.offsetWidth; hold.classList.add('pouring'); note.textContent='pouring';
    tm=setTimeout(complete,POUR); }
  function stop(){ if(done||!hold.classList.contains('pouring')) return; clearTimeout(tm); var p=Math.min(99,Math.round((performance.now()-t0)/POUR*100));
    hold.classList.remove('pouring'); hold.classList.add('retreat'); note.textContent='Released at '+p+'% — nothing written'; }
  hold.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){e.preventDefault();start();});
  hold.addEventListener('pointerup',stop); hold.addEventListener('pointerleave',stop); hold.addEventListener('pointercancel',stop);
  hold.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){ e.preventDefault(); if(done) return; if(!armed){armed=true;note.textContent='Enter again to seal';setTimeout(function(){if(!done){armed=false;note.textContent='disarmed — nothing written';}},3000);} else {complete();} } if(e.key==='Escape'){armed=false;note.textContent='disarmed — nothing written';} });
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
